### [27] [Unified Hallucination Fuzzing for Multimodal Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07525)
*Pengfei Zhou,Jiajun Song,Zhiwei Tang,Yixing Ma,Xiaopeng Peng,Donghui Si,Yuhang Xu,Huiqi Song,Yiyuan Miao,Yichen Qian,Weihua Chen,Wangbo Zhao,Bohan Zhuang,Jiasheng Tang,Yang You*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个系统性的多模态大模型（MLLM）幻觉评估框架，整合了细粒度基准UNIHALL和自适应模糊测试方法SAMF，揭示了最先进模型在动态测试下性能显著退化，并发现了"有帮助性-幻觉"权衡现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有幻觉评估主要依赖静态基准，存在分类覆盖狭窄和性能饱和的问题，难以反映模型在真实场景中的鲁棒性。幻觉仍是MLLM在高风险应用中可靠性的关键障碍，需要更全面的动态评估方法。

Method: 论文提出两大部分：1) UNIHALL，一个基于统一分类体系（涵盖对象、指令、知识三个维度）的细粒度幻觉数据集；2) SAMF（自适应性多模态模糊测试框架），采用进化突变策略探索模型幻觉边界，并集成基于多模态oracle的结构化评估指标体系以确保动态输入评估的可靠性。

Result: 实验表明，最先进的MLLM在模糊测试下相比传统设置表现出显著的性能退化，暴露了推理能力与事实基础之间的脱节。此外，发现了一个"有帮助性-幻觉"权衡：RL对齐会无意中加剧指令跟随任务中的谄媚行为。

Conclusion: 现有静态评估低估了MLLM的幻觉严重性；动态自适应评估更能揭示模型真实鲁棒性；RL对齐虽然在提升有帮助性方面有效，但可能以加剧幻觉为代价，需要在两者间寻求平衡。

Abstract: Hallucination remains a persistent challenge for Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs), severely limiting their reliability in high-stakes applications. Existing evaluations, predominantly based on static benchmarks, suffer from narrow taxonomical coverage and rapid performance saturation, failing to reflect model robustness in evolving real-world scenarios. To bridge this gap, we present a systematic evaluation framework integrating a comprehensive benchmark with self-evolving stress testing. First, we introduce UniHall, a fine-grained dataset grounded in a unified taxonomy spanning Object, Instruction, and Knowledge dimensions. Second, to address benchmark saturation, we propose Self-Adaptive Multimodal Fuzzing (SAMF), a self-adaptive framework that employs evolutionary mutation strategies to explore the boundaries of model hallucinations. Crucially, to ensure reliable assessment of dynamic inputs, SAMF incorporates a structured metric suite driven by an ensemble of multi-modal oracles. Our extensive experiments reveal that state-of-the-art MLLMs exhibit significant performance degradation under fuzzing compared to conventional settings, exposing a dissociation between reasoning capabilities and factual grounding. Furthermore, we identify a helpfulness-hallucination trade-off, where reinforcement learning alignment inadvertently exacerbates sycophancy in instruction-following tasks. The framework, code and benchmark are available at https://github.com/LanceZPF/EvalHall.

</details>


### [28] [DocAtlas: Long-Document Understanding as Mutable-State Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07527)
*Hongchen Wei,Yuanzhe Wang,Bei Liu,Yifan Yang,Qi Dai,Kai Qiu,Yunsheng Li,Dongdong Chen,Chong Luo,Zhenzhong Chen,Baining Guo*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: DocAtlas是一个将长文档理解视为可变状态信息搜索过程的系统，通过外部文档环境（harness）控制搜索、阅读、记笔记和回顾等工具，维护层次化文档树和笔记存储，在固定上下文预算下实现自我改进的检索、选择性证据访问和主动工作记忆。该系统既支持大型VLM的推理时使用，也支持紧凑型VLM的端到端强化学习训练。在MMLongBench-Doc上，使用GPT-5.4达到71.4%（超越人类专家基准65.8%），使用端到端RL训练的Qwen3.5-4B达到63.7%（相比54.4%的baseline大幅提升）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的检索增强系统通常从静态索引中在生成前选择证据，而近期的智能体系统虽增加了多轮工具调用但往往依赖冻结的专有模型且行为由提示词驱动。长文档理解需要跨页面、布局、表格、图表寻找并组合证据，现有方法缺乏可变的、可学习的文档信息访问机制。

Method: DocAtlas将长文档理解建模为可变状态的信息寻求过程，实例化为一个可变文档环境（mutable document harness）：该环境决定每一步搜索、读取、存储、回顾什么文档信息以及向模型展示什么内容。环境暴露搜索、阅读、记笔记和回顾等工具，维护层次化文档树和笔记存储，并在智能体记录证据时动态更新两者。系统结合了自我改进的检索、选择性证据访问和主动工作记忆，在固定上下文预算下运作。同一环境既支持大型VLM的推理时使用，也支持紧凑型VLM的端到端强化学习训练。

Result: 在MMLongBench-Doc基准上，DocAtlas配合GPT-5.4达到71.4%的准确率，超过人类专家参考水平65.8%；使用Qwen3.5-4B VLM通过DocAtlas环境进行端到端强化学习训练后达到63.7%，相比54.4%的直接输入baseline有大幅提升。

Conclusion: 可变文档环境（mutable document harness）设计能够显著提升紧凑型文档智能体的性能，且同一框架同时适用于大型VLM的推理和紧凑型VLM的RL训练，表明将长期文档理解外化为可更新状态环境是一种有效的系统设计范式。

Abstract: Long-document understanding requires models to find and combine evidence across many pages, layouts, tables, figures, and charts. Existing retrieval-augmented systems usually select evidence from a static index before generation, while recent agentic systems add multi-turn tool use but often rely on frozen proprietary backbones whose behavior is set by prompts. We present DocAtlas, a system that treats long-document understanding as a mutable-state information-seeking process. We instantiate DocAtlas as a mutable document harness: an external environment that determines what document information is searched, read, stored, reviewed, and shown to the model at each step. Given a document and question, the harness exposes search, reading, note-taking, and review tools, maintains a hierarchical tree and note store, and updates both as the agent records evidence. DocAtlas combines self-improving retrieval, selective evidence access, and active working memory under a fixed context budget. The same harness supports inference-time use with large VLMs and end-to-end reinforcement learning for compact VLM agents. With GPT-5.4, DocAtlas reaches 71.4\% on MMLongBench-Doc, exceeding the human-expert reference of 65.8\%. A Qwen3.5-4B VLM trained with end-to-end RL in the DocAtlas environment reaches 63.7\%, compared with a 54.4\% direct-input baseline, showing that mutable document-harness design can improve compact document agents by a large margin.

</details>


### [29] [WuYuEval: A Multi-Level Benchmark for Large Language Models in Solid Waste Management](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07529)
*Yi Zhang,Hongyang Wang,Zheng Hao Leong,Zihao Wu,Kaijun Lin,Zhixing Pan,Qixun Huangfu,Wei Ren,Wenyan Wu,Fangyun Wang,Wenting Yu,Hengyu Lin,Muling Yang,Zongguo Wen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: WuYuEval是一个面向固体废物管理（SWM）领域的大型语言模型（LLM）评估基准，包含基础模块（4,590道封闭式选择题）和专家模块（247道场景化开放式问答题），通过锚定校准的LLM-as-a-Judge评分与Elo配对比较对33个LLM进行评测。结果显示模型间性能差异显著，推理增强模式并非对所有模型都有正收益，仅在锚定于单位、假设和工程约束时有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM越来越多地被用作技术助手，但其在固体废物管理领域的专业能力难以评估，因为现有基准侧重通用知识而非工程、环境、政策约束下的专业决策。需要构建一个多层次的、覆盖专业决策能力的SWM评估基准。

Method: 构建WuYuEval基准，包含两层模块：基础模块（4,590道经质量审核的封闭式选择题，覆盖6种任务类型和8个领域类别）和专家模块（247道基于情境的开放式问答题，涉及多目标优化、约束权衡和系统设计）。评分采用锚定校准的LLM-as-a-Judge和Elo配对比较相结合的方式。对33个LLM进行系统评估，并检验推理模式（Thinking模式）对性能的影响。

Result: 领先模型在基础模块上达到94.64%准确率，但平均准确率从简单问题的84.14%降至困难问题的42.50%。性能短板集中在计算、实验设计、城市规划和开放式专家任务上。推理导向的Thinking模式在审计后改善大多数匹配模型对的表现，但收益取决于基线能力且并非均匀为正。

Conclusion: 可见的推理模式（Thinking模式）仅在锚定于单位、假设和工程约束时才有助益，否则可能导致偏离决策边界。WuYuEval既提供了一个评估资源，也为开发具备专业推理链和显式约束控制的SWM领域基础模型提供了实证基础。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used as technical assistants, but their competence in solid waste management (SWM) remains difficult to assess because existing benchmarks emphasize general knowledge rather than professional decisions under engineering, environmental, and policy constraints. We introduce WuYuEval, a multi-level benchmark for evaluating LLMs in SWM across foundational knowledge, domain reasoning, and expert decision-making. After quality auditing, WuYuEval contains a Foundation Module with 4,590 closed-ended multiple-choice questions across six task types and eight domain categories, together with an Expert Module with 247 scenario-based open-ended questions involving multi-objective optimization, constraint trade-offs, and system design. For expert tasks, we combine anchor-calibrated LLM-as-a-Judge scoring with Elo-based pairwise comparison. Across 33 LLMs, performance varied widely. The leading model reached 94.64\% accuracy on the Foundation Module, but average accuracy still fell from 84.14\% on easy questions to 42.50\% on hard questions, with lower performance concentrated in calculation, experimental design, urban planning, and open-ended expert tasks. Reasoning-oriented Thinking modes improve most matched model pairs after auditing, but the gains depend on baseline capability and are not uniformly positive. These results suggest that visible deliberation helps only when it remains anchored to units, assumptions, and engineering constraints; otherwise, it may drift from decisive answer boundaries. WuYuEval therefore provides both an evaluation resource and an empirical basis for developing SWM-oriented foundation models with professional reasoning chains and explicit constraint control.

</details>


### [30] [Search-G1: Grounded Search Agents via Representation-Based Intrinsic Rewards](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07531)
*Cheng Ruoxi,Ma Haoxuan,Zhang Hongyi,Zhang Junming,Duan Ranjie,Xia Qiaolin,Wang Hao,Lu Yu,Shi Haibo,Ma Xingjun*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: Search-G1 提出一种基于表征的内在奖励框架，通过两个干预校准的读出器（prompt-state readout 和 answer-commit readout）来衡量智能体答案的操作性（operational）grounding，在无需过程标注或 LLM 评审判别的条件下改善搜索智能体的 grounded 检索与搜索成本之间的权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 搜索增强语言智能体应在必要时才检索外部信息，并在检索证据上落实答案。现有外部奖励要么是稀疏的结果监督，要么依赖昂贵的过程标注或 LLM 评审判别；而基于策略侧信号（熵、似然、信息增益）的内部奖励虽然细粒度且廉价，但主要反映模型置信度而非证据 grounding。目标是在无需昂贵信号的前提下实现对"检索必要性"和"证据依赖度"的精确度量。

Method: Search-G1 提出两种干预校准（intervention-calibrated）的表征读出器：(1) prompt-state readout 预测闭卷充分性，其补集定义相对策略的检索必要性；(2) answer-commit readout 通过答案阶段对证据删除的敏感性估计证据依赖度。奖励为：当检索被判定为必要且答案对证据敏感时，为正确的检索轨迹提供额外信用；闭卷知识充分时偏好正确直接答案；并惩罚重复搜索。校准后奖励评分在策略优化阶段无需过程标注或 LLM-as-judge 推理。由于 RL 会改变策略表征，Search-G1 周期性地在最新检查点的轨迹上重新拟合两个读出器，使奖励与策略共同演化。

Result: 在多个基于搜索的问答基准和两种模型规模上的实验表明，Search-G1 改善了 grounding-搜索成本之间的权衡，在保持有竞争力的任务准确率的同时缩短了响应侧轨迹。

Conclusion: Search-G1 通过表征级、干预校准的内在奖励，在不依赖昂贵过程信号的情况下有效区分布置化检索与冗余搜索，为搜索增强智能体的高效策略优化提供了一条低成本、可扩展且随策略共同演化的奖励设计路径。

Abstract: Search-augmented language agents should retrieve external information only when necessary and ground their answers in retrieved evidence. Existing external rewards provide either sparse outcome supervision or richer feedback from process annotations and LLM judges. Outcome rewards scale readily but cannot distinguish grounded retrieval from redundant search, whereas richer signals require costly annotation or inference during training. Internal rewards based on policy-side signals such as entropy, likelihood, or information gain are graded and inexpensive to evaluate, yet mainly reflect model confidence rather than evidence grounding. We propose Search-G1, a representation-based intrinsic reward framework that measures the operational grounding of an agent's answers through two intervention-calibrated readouts. A prompt-state readout predicts closed-book sufficiency, whose complement defines policy-relative retrieval necessity; an answer-commit readout estimates evidence reliance from answer-stage sensitivity to evidence deletion. Together, they provide additional credit to correct searched trajectories when retrieval is estimated necessary and the answer is evidence-sensitive, favor correct direct answers when closed-book knowledge suffices, and penalize repeated search. After calibration, reward scoring requires neither process annotations nor LLM-as-judge inference during policy optimization. Because reinforcement learning changes policy representations, Search-G1 periodically refits both readouts on trajectories from the latest checkpoint, allowing the reward to co-evolve with the policy. Experiments across multiple search-based question-answering benchmarks and two model scales show that Search-G1 improves the grounding--search-cost trade-off, producing shorter response-side trajectories at competitive task accuracy. Code is available at https://github.com/Rosy0912/Search-G1.

</details>


### [31] [Scaling Inherently Interpretable Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07594)
*Guide Labs Team,Andreas Madsen,Aya Abdelsalam Ismail,Giang Nguyen,Isaac Plant,Muawiz Chaudhary,Nathaniel Monson,Saqib Azim,Zhichen Guo,Julius Adebayo*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文挑战了"可解释性是能力的代价"这一传统观点，提出将可解释性作为训练过程的约束条件与语言建模目标共同优化。作者发现，在三个数量级的计算规模下，可解释性不仅没有阻碍能力，反而随着规模增长而提升，模型表征变得更加解耦并与人类可理解的概念对齐。作者通过Steerling-8B实现了这一点——这是一个带有因果注意力掩码的扩散语言模型，能够对任意token组进行输入归因、概念归因和训练数据归因，支持闭环干预（诊断→检索→概念引导修正），且仅用1/2到1/16的计算量即可与开放同级别模型竞争。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统可解释性研究是事后进行的：模型先被训练为不透明系统，再通过可靠性难以确立的方法进行解释。作者质疑这一前提，认为可解释性不应被视为能力的"税收"（tax），而应被设计为训练流程本身的约束条件，从而在根源上解决可解释性问题。

Method: 将可解释性作为训练管线中的约束，与语言建模目标联合优化。构建Steerling-8B：一个带有因果注意力掩码的扩散语言模型，训练时强制模型表征解耦并与人类概念对齐，从而实现对任意生成token组的输入归因、概念归因和训练数据归因。

Result: 在自回归和扩散两种语言模型上，跨越三个计算量级（约3个数量级），可解释性与能力同步提升而非相互对抗。模型表征随规模增大更加解耦并与人类概念对齐。Steerling-8B仅用比开放同类模型少2-16倍的计算量即可保持竞争力，实现了闭环干预能力（诊断→检索→概念引导修正，无需重训练）。

Conclusion: 可解释性可以被设计进训练流程中，并且随规模增长而改善，而非牺牲能力。这提出了一种不同的扩展范式：可解释性与能力不是零和博弈，而是可以共同增长的。

Abstract: Interpretability is often treated as a tax on capability: language models are trained as opaque systems, then explained after the fact, with methods whose reliability is difficult to establish. In this work, we challenge this premise. Rather than reverse-engineering a model, we make interpretability a constraint of the training pipeline, optimized alongside the language modeling objective. Across three orders of magnitude of compute, on both autoregressive and diffusion language models, interpretability scales with capability rather than against it. Surprisingly, model representations become more disentangled and aligned with human-understandable concepts with scale.
  We instantiate the training-time recipe with Steerling-8B, a diffusion language model with a causal attention mask. For any group of generated tokens, Steerling-8B attributes the output to relevant input tokens, human-understandable concepts, and training data. This enables closed-loop intervention: diagnose an output through its concept or feature attribution, retrieve similar training data, and correct the behavior through concept steering without retraining. Steerling-8B remains competitive with open peer models trained on substantially 2-16x more compute, suggesting a different scaling paradigm: interpretability can be designed into training, and it improves with scale.

</details>


### [32] [Embedding Initialization for Unseen Low-resource Languages in Multilingual NMT: A Case Study on Limbum-English Translation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07629)
*Samiratu Ntohsi,Neza David Tuyishimire,Anesu Kafesu,Marvin Ogore,Samuel Oluwajunwonlo Babalola,Oche Ankeli*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一种多语言嵌入平均初始化的方法，用于在多语言神经机器翻译模型（如NLLB-200）中为未见语言选择代理语言标记，在Limbum-英语翻译任务上达到了与最佳单一代理语言相当的性能（chrF2++=46.7 vs 47.3），远优于零样本（12.5）和从头训练的Transformer（14.5）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: NLLB-200等多语言翻译模型仅覆盖200种语言，而喀麦隆的格拉斯菲尔德班图语等数千种语言未被支持。在微调这些模型处理未见语言时，从业者必须选择代理语言标记，但目前缺乏原则性的选择方法。

Method: 提出一种嵌入初始化策略：将多个与目标语言具有类型学关联且已存在于模型中的语言的嵌入取平均值，作为新语言标记的初始嵌入。在NLLB-200上使用8,837对Limbum-英语平行语料（来自新约文本和双语词典）进行微调评估。

Result: 多语言平均嵌入初始化达到chrF2++=46.7，与最佳单语言代理（斯瓦希里语代理，chrF2++=47.3）性能相当；两种NLLB-200变体均比从头训练的Transformer基线高出32个chrF2++点以上；但所有系统均未能保留声调变音符号。

Conclusion: 多语言迁移是极低资源班图语翻译中的主导因素，多语言嵌入平均初始化消除了启发式代理语言选择的必要性。然而，声调变音符号的保留仍是一个未解决的开放性挑战。

Abstract: Multilingual neural machine translation models such as NLLB-200 cover 200 languages but leave thousands unsupported, including most Grassfields Bantu languages of Cameroon. When fine-tuning these models for an unseen language, practitioners must choose a proxy language token, yet no principled method exists for this selection. We implemented an embedding initialization strategy where a language token is the average of embeddings from multiple typologically related languages already in the mod el. We evaluate this approach on Limbum-to-English translation using a parallel corpus of 8,837 sentence pairs from New Testament text and a bilingual dictionary. We compare models: NLLB-200 zero-shot (chrF2++ = 12.5), a Transformer trained from scratch (chrF2++ = 14.5), NLLB-200 fine-tuned with a Swahili proxy token (chrF2++ = 47.3), and NLLB-200 with our averaged embedding initialization (chrF2++ = 46.7). We find that the multi-language initialization achieves performance comparable to the best single-language proxy. Both NLLB-200 variants improve over the from-scratch baseline by over 32 chrF2++ points. These results show that multilingual transfer is the dominant factor in extremely low-resource Bantu translation while eliminating the need for heuristic proxy selection. However, all systems fail to preserve tonal diacritics, highlighting an open challenge. We make our dataset and code available to support further research.

</details>


### [33] [SurveyReview: A Reviewer-Aligned Benchmark for Survey Evaluators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07641)
*Yuheng Zhang,Yuanchun Wang,Fanjin Zhang,Ruyu Zhao,Juanzi Li,Jie Tang,Jing Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出SurveyReview，一个面向综述论文评审的多维度、评审者对齐的基准数据集，包含675篇综述论文和1630份评审报告，以及标准化训练/测试划分和评估协议；并开发了SurveyAlign基线评估器，通过对Qwen3-32B进行LoRA微调，在四个维度上将平均MSE从2.28降至1.38，MAE从1.15降至0.69，显著提升了与人类评审者的对齐程度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着大语言模型将综述写作自动化，可靠评估成为瓶颈，LLM被用作综述评审者。但现有方法大多依赖现成的LLM-as-a-judge方式，缺乏与人类评审者的系统对齐，也缺少量化对齐程度的系统性框架。

Method: 收集并标注675篇综述论文的1630份评审报告，将自由文本评论转化为四个维度（可读性、批判性、全面性、结构）的评分及支持性理由；发布标准化训练/测试划分和评估协议；基于Qwen3-32B通过LoRA微调构建SurveyAlign基线评估器，并结合外部知识增强知识密集型维度。

Result: 在测试集上，SurveyAlign相较于基于GPT-5.2的提示式评审，在所有四个维度上将平均MSE从2.28降至1.38（减少39.5%），MAE从1.15降至0.69（减少40%），显著提升了与人类评审者的对齐度。

Conclusion: 本文首次建立了多维度、评审者对齐的综述评审数据集及可复现评估框架，并开发了与人类评审者对齐程度显著提升的强基线评估器，为未来研究提供了竞争性参考。代码和数据已公开。

Abstract: The rapid advancement of large language models has transformed survey writing from a months-long manual effort into an automated process. As generation scales, reliable evaluation becomes the bottleneck, and LLMs are increasingly used as survey evaluators. However, existing approaches largely rely on off-the-shelf LLM-as-a-judge methods without systematic alignment to human reviewers, and there remains a lack of systematic frameworks for quantifying alignment with human reviewers. To address this gap, we propose SurveyReview, a reviewer-aligned, multi-dimensional benchmark and dataset for survey evaluation. We collect and annotate 675 survey papers with 1,630 review reports. We structure authentic peer-review reports by converting free-form comments into four-dimensional scores (Readability, Criticalness, Comprehensiveness, Structure) paired with supporting rationales. We further release standardized train/test splits and an evaluation protocol to measure alignment between automatic evaluators and human reviewers. To validate the benchmark, we develop SurveyAlign, a strong baseline evaluator by fine-tuning Qwen3-32B with LoRA on our annotated data, augmented with external knowledge for knowledge-intensive dimensions. On the test set, SurveyAlign substantially improves reviewer alignment over prompt-based judging with GPT-5.2, reducing average MSE from 2.28 to 1.38 and MAE from 1.15 to 0.69 across all four dimensions. Our contributions are twofold: (1) we establish the first multi-dimensional, reviewer-aligned dataset with a reproducible evaluation framework for survey reviewing; (2) we develop a strong baseline evaluator that substantially improves alignment with human reviewers, providing a competitive reference for future research. Our code and data are available at https://surveyreview.github.io

</details>


### [34] [Evaluating Dedicated Monolingual and Joint Multilingual Causal Models for Dravidian Languages](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07727)
*Venkata Naga Sai Vishnu Rohit Pulipaka*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文从头训练了五个GPT-2架构模型，对比四种达罗毗荼语（泰米尔语、泰卢固语、卡纳达语、马拉雅拉姆语）的单语模型与一个共享词表的四语言多语模型，发现单语模型在情感分类和命名实体识别上优于mGPT，且其分词器在各语言上均比共享多语模型更高效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 达罗毗荼语系语言在多语言预训练模型中仅占极小比例的训练数据，导致这些模型对这些语言的真实能力不明确。作者希望系统性地评估单语模型与多语模型在低资源语言上的性能差异。

Conclusion: 对于达罗毗荼语这类低资源语言，从头训练的单语模型比现有大规模多语言模型更具优势，单语分词器和专用模型能够更有效地处理这些语言。

Abstract: Dravidian languages, mainly Tamil, Telugu, Kannada, and Malayalam make up only a small part of the data used to train multilingual language models, so it's not clear how much per-language ability these models actually keep. I have trained five GPT-2 architecture models from scratch to compare four monolingual models (one each for Tamil, Telugu, Kannada, and Malayalam, each with its own 32K-vocabulary subword tokenizer) against one multilingual model sharing a 64K-vocabulary subword tokenizer across all four languages. All the 5 models are trained on cleaned CC-100, Wikipedia, and Samanantar data. I have tested the models on perplexity, bits-per-byte, tokenizer efficiency, and fine-tuning results which are compared against mGPT. The monolingual models outperform mGPT on sentiment classification and named entity recognition, and their tokenizers proved more efficient than the shared multilingual model across all the languages tested.

</details>

*Elnaserledinellah Mahmoud Abdelwahab*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文通过建立阿拉伯语非连接形态学的形式化数学模型，证明索绪尔任意性假设与古典阿拉伯语的结构架构不兼容，并利用条件Kolmogorov复杂度形式化相对任意性，证明阿拉伯语相对任意性是可判定的且小于1，而一般相对任意性是不可判定的，从而建立了阿拉伯语与印欧语言之间的系统复杂度不对称性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 后现代主义主张无限制的语义不确定性，其哲学基础是索绪尔符号任意性公理。作者旨在检验这一主张是否与古典阿拉伯语的结构形态学（非连接形态学）相兼容，为语言任意性理论提供一个形式化、可计算的验证框架。

Method: 方法包括：(1) 构建阿拉伯语非连接形态学的形式数学模型，其中词汇意义由不变词根与形态句法模式的交互决定；(2) 建立形态对应定理，证明每个词项由词根-模式对唯一生成；(3) 建立语义定位定理，证明词汇意义在派生层面（表面实现之前）确定；(4) 用条件Kolmogorov复杂度将索绪尔的相对任意性形式化为"无规则性质"；(5) 对通用相对任意性不可判定性和阿拉伯语相对任意性可判定性进行形式证明。

Result: 主要结果包括：(1) 证明了通用相对任意性（general relative arbitrariness）在形式上是不可判定的；(2) 证明了阿拉伯语相对任意性是可判定的，且其动机性符号（W层和M层）的相对任意性可验证地小于1；(3) 建立了阿拉伯语与印欧语言之间的严格系统复杂度不对称性。

Conclusion: 结论认为，后现代主义关于无限制语义不确定性的主张以及索绪尔任意性公理，在古典阿拉伯语的结构架构下不成立。阿拉伯语的非连接形态学提供了可判定的、受限的语义确定性，其相对任意性在算法意义上低于印欧语言，挑战了语言任意性的普适性主张。

Abstract: This paper investigates whether the postmodern claim of unrestricted semantic indeterminacy, and its foundational Saussurean axiom of the arbitrary sign, are compatible with the structural architecture of Classical Arabic. We develop a formal mathematical model of Arabic non concatenative morphology in which lexical meaning is determined by the interaction between an invariant root and a morphosyntactic pattern. Within this framework, we establish a Morphological Correspondence Theorem, demonstrating that every lexical item is uniquely generated by a root pattern pair, and a Semantic Localization Theorem, proving that lexical meaning is determined at the derivational level prior to surface realization. To address Saussurean weaker notion of relative arbitrariness, we formalize it via conditional Kolmogorov complexity, defining arbitrariness algorithmically as the no rule property. We prove that general relative arbitrariness is formally undecidable, while Arabic relative arbitrariness is decidable and provably less than 1 for its motivated signifiers (Levels W and M), establishing a strict system complexity asymmetry over Indo-European languages.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 本文构建PoVisLE数据集，这是专为波兰设计的单文化视觉语言基准。数据包含1,117张图像和2,366个手工标注的VQA对，采用"接地评估"（grounded evaluation）范式，即语言在视觉情境交互中进行解释，评估模型对区域特定含义、符号内容和上下文相关视觉线索的深层理解。

Result: PoVisLE提供了一个受控且具有挑战性的资源，用于评估模型在文化接地视觉语言理解中的表现，超越了表面级识别的能力。

Conclusion: 作者认为PoVisLE填补了现有文化基准的空白，为研究和评估VLMs在波兰单文化情境下的多模态理解能力提供了可靠的评测资源，有助于推动文化感知视觉语言模型的发展。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) have achieved strong performance on tasks such as image captioning, visual question answering, and image-to-text generation. However, they are predominantly trained on English-centric data, which limits their ability to handle culturally grounded visual understanding and leads to failures in interpreting region-specific meanings, symbolic content, and context-dependent visual cues. Existing benchmarks for cultural competence are often template-driven and focused on surface-level recognition, making them insufficient for evaluating deeper linguistic and pragmatic understanding in culturally situated settings. We introduce PoVisLE, a monocultural vision-language benchmark for Polish designed to evaluate culturally grounded multimodal understanding under a grounded evaluation paradigm, where language is interpreted in interaction with visual context. The dataset contains 1,117 images and 2,366 manually annotated VQA pairs. Overall, our dataset provides a controlled and challenging resource for assessing culturally grounded vision-language understanding beyond surface-level recognition.

</details>


### [37] [On the use of foundation models in cognitive science](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07812)
*Raj Sanjay Shah,Alex Warstadt,Michael Frank,Sashank Varma*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个四阶段推断框架，用于评估基础模型（FMs）作为认知和发展模型的有效性，强调仅凭借行为对齐不足以支撑科学结论，必须嵌入明确的理论承诺、理论诊断性任务和系统对比评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近年来大量研究评估基础模型在认知和发展层面与人类行为的一致性，但将这些模型用作候选认知模型面临重大的方法论和概念挑战。核心问题是：在什么条件下，行为对齐能够证明将FMs视为认知解释模型是合理的？现有研究缺乏系统框架来回答这一问题。

Method: 作者提出并阐述了一个四阶段推断框架：（1）将人类实验任务改编为模型兼容格式；（2）明确链接假设，将模型输出映射到人类行为测量指标；（3）评估行为对应性；（4）在候选模型或操作之间进行对比评估。同时澄清了链接假设在映射模型输出与人类行为指标中的作用，识别了限制对齐声明有效性的挑战，并提出了理论驱动和比较评估的原则。

Result: 论文的核心结论是行为拟合本身不足以支撑科学有效性。只有当对齐被嵌入明确的理论承诺、理论诊断性任务和跨候选模型的系统性对比评估中时，行为对齐才具有科学意义。论文提供了指导性的框架和原则来指导未来FMs认知建模研究。

Conclusion: 行为对齐不能单独作为FMs被视为认知模型的充分条件；需要四阶段推断框架结合理论约束和对比评估，才能赋予对齐结果以科学的解释力。该框架为未来基础模型的认知和发展评估提供了方法论指南。

Abstract: A host of recent studies have evaluated the cognitive and developmental alignment of Foundation Models (FMs). These investigations include evaluations of their correspondence to adult performance across a range of cognitive domains, as well as whether aspects of model training track children's cognitive development. However, using FMs as candidate cognitive models poses significant methodological and conceptual challenges. A key question underlies this effort: under what conditions does behavioral alignment justify treating FMs as explanatory models of cognition? In this paper, we articulate a four-stage inferential framework for evaluating FMs as cognitive and developmental models: adapting human experimental tasks to model-compatible formats, specifying linking hypotheses that map model outputs to human measures, evaluating behavioral correspondence, and comparing across candidate models or manipulations. We clarify the role of linking hypotheses in mapping model outputs to human behavioral measures, identify challenges that constrain alignment claims, and propose principles for theory-driven and comparative evaluation. Throughout, we argue that behavioral fit alone is insufficient. Alignment becomes scientifically meaningful only when embedded within explicit theoretical commitments, theory-diagnostic tasks, and systematic contrastive evaluation across candidate models.

</details>

*Adelaide Danilov,Aria Nourbakhsh,Oleksandr Marchenko Breneur,Salima Lamsiyah*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究语言模型内部如何表征"说话者"（助手、角色扮演人设、叙述故事角色），利用稀疏自编码器（SAE）在回合边界和代词标记位置提取特征，通过过滤流水线筛选不同深度的特征，并分析其特征的引导效应与激活分布。核心发现：助手与角色扮演人设并非独立替代关系——人设保留助手相关的特征核心，但在网络深层逐渐分化（从操作机制到行为/风格特征）；故事角色则不包含助手特征核心。角色扮演和故事均可通过"沉浸式模拟模式"与助手区分开，但助手在某些情况下即使默认设置下也会进入或缓慢漂移进入该模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型内部如何表征"谁在说话"这一问题尚未被充分探索。理解不同生成设置（助手、角色扮演、故事叙述）下的说话主体内部表征，对揭示模型的行为机制、角色一致性以及安全可控性具有重要意义。

Method: 使用用户表达情感文本与对应模型响应的数据集，将三种生成设置（Assistant, Roleplay, Story）分解为在回合边界和代词标记位置提取的稀疏自编码器（SAE）特征，通过过滤流水线在不同网络深度进行特征筛选，并对每个存活特征进行引导效应（steering effects）和激活分布的表征分析。

Result: 主要发现：（1）助手与角色扮演人设并非独立替代关系，角色扮演保留助手的核心特征，但在不同网络层逐步分化，从操作机制特征到行为与风格特征；（2）生成的故事角色缺乏助手相关特征核心；（3）角色扮演和故事均可通过"沉浸式模拟模式"（Immersive Simulation Mode）与助手设置区分；（4）即使默认设置下，助手有时也会进入或缓慢漂移进入该沉浸式模拟模式。

Conclusion: 语言模型中的说话者表征呈现层级分化结构：助手与角色扮演人设共享特征核心并随深度逐渐分化，故事角色则独立于助手核心。此外，沉浸式模拟模式作为一种关键区分维度，不仅可用于分离不同说话者设置，还揭示了助手在默认条件下也可能发生潜在的状态漂移，这对理解模型角色控制的稳定性与安全性有启示意义。

Abstract: How a language model internally represents who is speaking, the Assistant, an assigned roleplay persona, or a narrated story character, remains underexplored. We study speaker representations using a dataset of user-expressed emotional text and corresponding model responses. We decompose three generation settings (Assistant, Roleplay, and Story) into sparse autoencoder features extracted at turn-boundary and pronoun-token positions and selected through a filtering pipeline for different depths. We characterize each surviving feature through its steering effects and activation distribution. Our main finding is that the Assistant and roleplay personas are not independent alternatives: personas retain the Assistant-associated feature core while progressively differentiating from it across layers, starting from operational machinery towards behavioral and stylistic features. Meanwhile, generated story characters lack the Assistant-associated core. Both Story and Roleplay can be distinguished from the Assistant with Immersive Simulation Mode. However, the Assistant can sometimes enter or slowly drift into it even in the default setting.

</details>


### [39] [SurakshaEval: An Indic Safety Benchmark for Multilingual LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07862)
*Debopriyo Banerjee,Kapil Rajesh Kavitha,Angana Borah,Xudong Han,Yuxia Wang,Parameswari Krishnamurthy,Utkarsh Agarwal,Atharva Kulkarni,Swaran Lata,Ayush Munot,Dhruv Sahnan,Aaryamonvikram Singh,Preslav Nakov,Monojit Choudhury*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: SurakshaEval是一个针对十种主要印度语言及英语的新型安全基准测试，包含人工编写的、覆盖真实场景的提示词，用于评估多语言大语言模型在不同语言和文化背景下的安全性能。研究发现即使是强大的多语言LLM在印度语言（尤其是本地文字）下也难以满足细微的安全要求，并识别出过拒绝、隐含偏见漏检和区域敏感情境下上下文感知不足等系统性失败模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的大语言模型安全评估数据集主要集中于英语和西方语境，忽视了其他语言中的语言多样性和文化特定的安全风险，导致多语言模型在非英语环境下的安全性缺乏可靠的评估工具。

Method: 构建SurakshaEval基准，包含人工编写的提示词（涵盖通用提示和区域/语言特定提示），针对十种主要印度语言及英语，并对多种最先进LLM进行基准测试，建立基线安全性能并分析失败模式。

Result: 强大多语言LLM在印度语言（尤其是本地文字书写的提示）下难以可靠地满足细微的安全要求；发现的主要失败模式包括过拒绝、对隐含偏见的漏检，以及区域敏感情境下上下文感知不足。

Conclusion: 研究强调迫切需要将区域特定数据和结构化评估协议纳入安全评估框架，以支持符合多样化社会价值观、安全且合乎道德地开发和部署AI系统。

Abstract: Existing safety evaluation datasets for large language models (LLMs) predominantly focus on English and Western contexts, often overlooking the linguistic diversity and culturally grounded safety risks present in other languages. To address this gap, we introduce SurakshaEval, a novel safety benchmark composed of human-written prompts spanning real-world scenarios, explicitly designed for ten major Indian languages - Assamese, Bengali, Gujarati, Hindi, Kannada, Malayalam, Marathi, Punjabi, Tamil, and Telugu, along with English. SurakshaEval includes both generic prompts common across India and region- and language-specific prompts that capture localized sociocultural sensitivities. We benchmark a broad range of state-of-the-art LLMs on SurakshaEval, establish baseline safety performance, and identify recurring failure modes, including over-refusal, missed detection of implicit bias, and insufficient contextual awareness in regionally sensitive settings. Our results show that even strong multilingual LLMs struggle to reliably meet nuanced safety requirements when operating in Indic languages, particularly in native scripts. These findings highlight the urgent need for safety evaluation frameworks that incorporate region-specific data and structured assessment protocols, enabling the development and deployment of AI systems that operate securely, ethically, and in alignment with diverse societal values. Our code and data are available at https://github.com/debobanerjee/SurakshaEval. Warning: This paper contains text that may be offensive or unsafe.

</details>


### [40] [Detection of Self-Introductions in Legislative Testimony](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07891)
*Sofija Dimitrijevic,Pallavi Das,Kasey Liu,Foaad Khosmood*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于检测立法委员会证词中自我介绍并提取发言人姓名的机器学习流水线，使用三种分类器（决策树、随机森林、XGBoost）对154万条话语进行训练，XGBoost达到F1 0.9747，引入BERT概率特征后进一步将F1提升至0.9782。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在立法委员会证词中，自我介绍非常常见。成功检测并提取发言人的姓名对于政府会议场景下的说话人识别任务具有极大帮助。

Method: 从五个州立法会议收集154万条话语构建训练数据集，使用姓名匹配启发式方法自动生成标签，构建包含词袋、位置上下文、结构信号、介绍短语指示符和话语上下文特征的特征集，训练决策树、随机森林和XGBoost三种分类器。作为扩展，使用微调后的BERT分类器对完整候选数据集进行评分，并将BERT概率输出作为额外特征。

Result: XGBoost表现最佳，F1分数为0.9747，错误数最少；加入微调BERT概率特征后，F1提升至0.9782，总测试错误从241降至207。相比决策树基线（F1 0.9323），主要增益来自话语上下文特征和boosting集成策略；BERT提供适度的补充信号。错误分析显示少数误报实为因源数据姓名不一致而被错误标注的真实自我介绍，表明实测指标略低于真实性能。

Conclusion: 本文提出的流水线能有效检测立法证词中的自我介绍并提取发言人姓名，XGBoost搭配BERT概率特征是最优组合。由于部分"误报"实际是标签噪声导致的，真实模型性能优于表面指标所呈现的水平。

Abstract: Self-introductions are common in legislative committee testimonies. Successfully detecting them and extracting the speaker's name is enormously helpful in the task of speaker identification in the context of government meetings. In this paper, we present a pipeline for detection of self-introductions in legislative committee testimony using machine learning. We construct a training dataset from 1.54 million utterances spanning five state legislative sessions, apply a name-matching heuristic to generate automatic labels, and train three classifiers: a decision tree, random forest, and XGBoost to find self-introductions and extract the speaker's name. We construct a feature set combining bag-of-words, positional context, structural signals, introductory phrase indicators, and discourse context features. Among the three classifiers, XGBoost achieves the best performance with an F1 score of 0.9747 and the fewest total errors; adding fine-tuned BERT probability features improves this further. As an extension, we score the full candidate dataset with a fine-tuned BERT classifier and add BERT probability outputs as features. This BERT-augmented XGBoost model improves F1 from 0.9747 to 0.9782 and reduces total test errors from 241 to 207. The primary gain over the decision tree baseline (F1 0.9323) is driven by discourse context features and the boosting ensemble strategy; BERT provides a modest complementary signal. Analysis of false positives reveals that a minority are genuine self-introductions mislabeled due to name inconsistencies in the source data, indicating that measured metrics modestly understate true performance.

</details>


### [41] [Thinking Hard, Not Smart: Reasoning Models Fail to Ration Test-Time Compute Across Questions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07968)
*Chenrui Fan,Yize Cheng,Ming Li,Yongyuan Liang,Tianyi Zhou,Soheil Feizi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一个"考试式"评估框架，研究推理语言模型如何在多道题之间分配共享的推理计算预算（token预算），以最大化总分。研究发现包括开源和前沿推理模型在内的多个模型无法跨题目战略性地分配预算，表现为贪婪的顺序求解行为——按呈现顺序优先处理题目、在前面的题目上过度投入、对题目分值不敏感，且随题目数量增加而加剧。显式规划提示能更均匀地分配计算但无法产生价值或难度感知的优先级排序，该行为模式从数学推理扩展到代码推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对推理语言模型的评估通常只研究单次一个问题的测试时计算，但实际应用中多个问题往往共享端到端的成本或延迟约束。模型需要决定如何在有限的推理计算预算内跨问题分配，这一能力（全局预算分配）尚未被现有评估框架捕捉，急需一个能研究此场景的评估框架。

Method: 引入一个考试式（exam-style）评估框架：模型必须在不同难度和不同分值的问题之间分配一个共享的token预算以最大化总分。在多个开源和前沿推理模型上评估，并通过改变题目数量、分值权重、显式规划提示（planning prompts）和不同推理域（数学与代码）来系统分析模型的预算分配行为。

Result: 模型在预算分配上表现不佳，呈贪婪顺序求解器行为：按呈现顺序优先级排序、前端加载（在前面的题目投入过多）、对分值不敏感；这些倾向在题目数量增多时更显著。显式规划提示使计算分配更均匀但并未产生价值或难度感知的优先级。相同行为模式在数学推理和代码推理中均出现。

Conclusion: 全局预算分配是一个独立的、有别于传统逐题评估所捕捉的能力维度，当前推理模型在此方面仍然存在挑战。这一发现为推理模型的评估与改进提供了新方向。

Abstract: Reasoning language models increasingly use test-time compute to improve performance, but existing evaluations typically study this compute one question at a time. Yet when multiple problems share an end-to-end cost or latency constraint, models must decide how to divide limited inference compute among them. We introduce an exam-style evaluation framework for studying this setting, in which a model must distribute one shared token budget across questions with different difficulty and point values to maximize its total score. Across several open and frontier reasoning models, we find that models fail to allocate a shared budget strategically across questions of varying difficulties and values. Models behave largely as greedy sequential solvers: they prioritize questions by presentation order, front-load effort on early questions, and remain insensitive to value, with these tendencies becoming more pronounced as the number of questions grows. Explicit planning prompts spread compute more evenly but do not produce value- or difficulty-aware prioritization. The same behavioral pattern extends from mathematical to code reasoning. These findings establish global budget allocation as a distinct capability that is not captured by conventional per-question evaluation and remains a challenge for current reasoning models.

</details>


### [42] [Prompt Embedding Probes (PEP): Hallucination Detection in LLMs from Hidden States](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08024)
*Zakhar Mrykhin,Valentin Malykh*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出Prompt Embedding Probes (PEP)，一种基于冻结LLM隐藏状态的白盒幻觉检测方法，通过向标准线性探针输入中注入少量可学习的prompt embeddings，在TriviaQA、GSM8K和MedQA数据集上使用Qwen3模型验证其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型虽能生成流畅且有用的回答，但仍容易产生幻觉（hallucination）。现有基于隐藏状态的幻觉检测方法（如标准线性探针）性能有限，需要一种更具表达能力且参数高效的检测方法，在不微调骨干网络的前提下提升幻觉检测的准确性。

Method: PEP方法扩展了标准线性探针，通过在探针输入中添加少量可学习的prompt embeddings来实现答案级别的幻觉检测。该方法作为白盒方法，从冻结的LLM隐藏状态中提取信号，仅添加少量可训练参数。评估使用Qwen3模型的多规模变体，在TriviaQA、GSM8K和MedQA三个数据集上进行。

Result: 在主要分布内（in-distribution）设置下，PEP相比标准线性探针在隐藏状态检测上有所提升。在预生成预测（pre-generation）和跨模型迁移（cross-model transfer）设置下PEP依然有效，但跨数据集迁移（cross-dataset transfer）的鲁棒泛化仍具挑战。

Conclusion: 基于prompt的自适应方法能够增强隐藏状态探针的检测能力，同时保持骨干网络冻结并仅增加少量可训练参数。该工作证明了一种参数高效的白盒幻觉检测技术路线的可行性。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) can generate fluent and useful responses but remain prone to hallucinations. We introduce Prompt Embedding Probes (PEP), a white-box method for answer-level hallucination detection from the hidden states of a frozen LLM. PEP extends standard linear probes by augmenting the input with a small number of learnable prompt embeddings. We evaluate PEP on TriviaQA, GSM8K, and MedQA using Qwen3 models at multiple scales. PEP improves hidden-state-based detection over standard linear probes in the main in-distribution setting. We further evaluate PEP for pre-generation prediction, cross-model transfer, and out-of-distribution generalization. PEP remains effective in the pre-generation and cross-model settings, whereas robust cross-dataset transfer remains difficult. These results show that prompt-based adaptation can strengthen hidden-state probing while keeping the backbone frozen and adding only a small number of trainable parameters.

</details>


### [43] [APEX-VW: A Document-Level English-Spanish Post-Editing Dataset in the Healthcare Domain](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08059)
*Marie Escribe,Tharindu Ranasinghe,Amal Haddad Haddad,Hansi Hettiarachchi,Damith Premasiri*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文介绍APEX-VW语料库，一个基于英国NHS虚拟病房文档和Trados Studio专业译后编辑构建的开放英西平行语料库，共42k词、7篇文档，涵盖四种MT系统输出，保留文档顺序和CAT工具上下文，为文档级自动译后编辑（APE）和术语规范化、修正传播研究提供基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动译后编辑（APE）语料大多基于句子级别或合成数据，无法真实反映专业翻译工作流中术语和词汇修正如何在文档内跨片段传播的现象，缺乏适合研究文档级APE和修正传播的语料资源。

Method: 从NHS虚拟病房文档中选取七篇文档共42k词源文本，使用四种代表不同范式的MT系统翻译成西班牙语，再由专业译者在Trados Studio中进行受控译后编辑，记录术语、质量保证等设置，构建保留文档顺序和CAT工具上下文信息的新型EN-ES语料库。

Result: 构建了APEX-VW语料库，与WMT APE、eSCAPE、MLQE-PE和LangMark等既有资源相比，该语料库保留了文档级结构信息和CAT工作流上下文，适用于术语规范化、修正传播和人机协同翻译支持等研究方向。

Conclusion: 该语料库可作为文档级APE和传播感知辅助工具的基准资源，推动APE研究从句子级向文档级发展。

Abstract: Post-Editing (PE) of Machine Translation (MT) output often involves repeating the same lexical and terminological corrections across many segments, especially in specialised and highly repetitive documents. Despite substantial work on Automatic Post-Editing (APE), most available corpora operate at the sentence level, others are synthetic, and overall not designed to study how corrections propagate in realistic Computer-Assisted Translation (CAT) workflows. This paper presents the APEX-VW (Automatic Post-Editing eXperiments on Virtual Wards) Corpus, a new open English-Spanish (EN-ES) dataset built from recent NHS virtual-ward documents and professional PE in Trados Studio, with controlled MT, terminology, and quality assurance settings. The corpus contains seven document-coherent source texts totalling 42k words, translated with four MT systems representing different paradigms and then post-edited by professional translators. Unlike prior resources such as WMT APE corpora, eSCAPE, MLQE-PE, or LangMark, the dataset preserves document order and CAT-tool context, making it suitable for research on terminology normalisation, correction propagation, and human-in-the-loop translation support. The paper describes the corpus design, data preparation, PE setup, and initial corpus statistics, and positions the resource as a benchmark for document-level APE and propagation-aware assistive tools.

</details>


### [44] [DialectS2S: End-to-End Speech Dialogue Modeling for Low-Resource Chinese Dialects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08067)
*Yi Shu,Tianyu Peng,Yingzhuo Deng,Wen Yang,Jun Lin,Changming Xie,Xinyu Yu,Jiajun Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出DialectS2S，一个面向中文方言的端到端语音对话模型。该方法通过可扩展的方言语音对话合成管道构建训练数据，并引入两阶段后训练策略和自对齐语音监督，解决方言适应过程中隐藏表示与语音目标之间语义不一致的问题。实验表明DialectS2S在多个中文方言上优于现有基线，显著提升方言一致性、响应质量和语音可懂度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 端到端语音对话模型主要面向主流语言优化，在低资源方言场景表现受限，原因是方言语音数据稀缺。此外，在方言适应过程中，模型的语义表示空间持续演变，而传统语音监督保持不变，导致隐藏表示与语音目标之间存在语义不一致，降低语音稳定性和自然度。

Method: 1）构建可扩展的方言语音对话合成管道，用于高效数据构造；2）提出两阶段后训练策略，配合自对齐语音监督，使语音监督的语义内容与模型演进后的语义表示对齐，提升方言语音生成质量。

Result: DialectS2S在多个中文方言的语音对话任务上一致优于现有基线，在方言一致性、响应质量和语音可懂度方面均取得显著提升，并提供高效可扩展的低资源方言端到端语音对话建模方案。

Conclusion: DialectS2S为低资源方言场景下的端到端语音对话建模提供了高效可扩展的解决方案，且已全面开源框架（含模型检查点、训练数据集和微调代码），以促进后续研究与应用。

Abstract: Current end-to-end speech dialogue models are primarily optimized for mainstream languages and remain limited in low-resource dialect scenarios due to the scarcity of dialect speech data. Moreover, during dialect adaptation, the semantic representation space of speech dialogue models continuously evolves, while conventional speech supervision remains unchanged, leading to semantic inconsistency between hidden representations and speech targets and degrading speech stability and naturalness. To address these issues, we propose DialectS2S, an end-to-end speech dialogue model for Chinese dialects. We first develop a scalable dialect speech dialogue synthesis pipeline for efficient data construction. We further introduce a two-stage post-training strategy with self-aligned speech supervision, which aligns the semantic content of speech supervision with the evolved semantic representations of the model to improve dialect speech generation quality. Experimental results show that DialectS2S consistently outperforms existing baselines across multiple Chinese dialects in speech dialogue, achieving substantial improvements in dialect consistency, response quality, and speech intelligibility. Our work provides an efficient and scalable solution for end-to-end speech dialogue modeling in low-resource dialect scenarios. To facilitate future research and practical applications, we fully open-source the DialectS2S framework, including model checkpoints, training datasets, and fine-tuning code.

</details>


### [45] [Commitment Before Realization: When Classifier-Free Guidance Becomes Unnecessary in Masked Diffusion Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08082)
*Fan Zhou,Weitian Wang,Tim Van de Cruys*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了masked diffusion语言模型解码中classifier-free guidance (CFG)的必要性，提出"承诺地平线"(commitment horizon)概念，即从某点开始切换到base模型不会显著降低最终约束满足率的临界时刻。研究发现CFG的价值高度依赖于提示词，且多数情况下收益集中在早期；在每个提示词自定义的cross-fitted地平线处冻结CFG，在所有13个子任务上与全程CFG相当甚至更优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CFG在masked diffusion语言模型解码中通常全程启用，但其收益在不同提示词之间和不同解码阶段差异显著。作者希望回答"CFG何时真正必要"这一核心问题，从而减少不必要的计算开销并提升解码效率。

Method: 作者从任意部分输出出发，比较在继续使用CFG与仅使用base模型续写两种情况下的最终约束满足概率，二者差异定义guidance的"剩余价值"。定义承诺地平线为切换到base模型导致最终成功率下降不超过容忍阈值的最早时刻。利用committor（base模型下的约束满足概率）作为鞅的性质，分析CFG每步效应的主导项为guidance logit方向与后继committor之间的协方差，从而给出guidance何时有效的局部刻画。

Result: CFG依赖具有高度提示特异性：许多提示词无需CFG即可成功；对确实受益的提示词，增益集中在解码早期。承诺地平线通常很早出现，且在约束族内部的变化大于族间差异。使用每个提示词cross-fitted的地平线冻结CFG，在所有13个子任务上均不劣于全程CFG，即使仍有大量token被mask。此外，在后段区域增加并行度对约束成功影响很小（但流利度仍随并行宽度下降），而对失败轨迹重新打开已提交位置可改善两种失败模式的恢复。

Conclusion: CFG并非全程必需，其价值集中在早期间歇。将解码过程拆分为"承诺阶段"与"实现阶段"，在恰当的初始阶段结束后切换到base模型解码，可以在保持约束满足性能的同时提高效率。这一发现为masked diffusion模型的解码策略提供了理论依据和实用指导。

Abstract: Classifier-free guidance (CFG) is usually kept on throughout masked diffusion language model decoding, although its benefit varies across prompts and over time. We study when CFG is actually needed by comparing, from any partial output, the probability of eventual constraint satisfaction under continued CFG and under base-only continuation. Their difference defines the remaining value of guidance. Guidance dependence is highly prompt-specific. Many prompts already succeed without CFG, while for others it provides no measurable benefit or can be harmful. For prompts that do benefit, the gain is often concentrated early. We define the commitment horizon $\astar$ as the earliest point from which switching all remaining decoding to the base model reduces final success by no more than a chosen tolerance. Under the base model, the corresponding success probability, or committor, is a martingale. To first order, CFG's per-step effect is governed by the covariance between the guidance logit direction and the successor committor. This gives a local account of when guidance can help, but it does not by itself locate the horizon. Among prompts with an observed preterminal horizon, $\astar$ is usually early and varies more within constraint families than between them. Freezing each prompt at its own cross-fitted horizon is noninferior to full CFG on all 13 subtasks at the prespecified margin, even while many tokens remain masked. This separates commitment from realization. The boundary also identifies a later region in which higher parallelism adds only a small cost in constraint success, although fluency still degrades with parallel width. For failed trajectories, reopening committed positions improves recovery in both failure modes.

</details>


### [46] [Archer: Adaptive Reuse of Cached Hidden States for Efficient Rollback in Diffusion Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08086)
*Xuning He,Zinan Sheng,Yongding Tao,Huanyu Liu,Ge Li,Xue Jiang,Yihong Dong*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: Archer是一种用于支持回滚的扩散语言模型(DLM)的免训练KV缓存方法，通过不对称地缓存提示词的K/V状态（在受限状态邻域内重复使用）同时保持可变响应状态同步，实现了2.57倍平均加速和更好的生成质量（平均33.63%性能，Pass@1提升最高3.05个百分点）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散语言模型通过迭代精炼序列实现回滚能力，但每次去噪更新都会改变全局上下文，迫使提示词和响应状态都被重新计算。传统KV缓存假设历史状态不可变，难以与回滚机制兼容。现有DLM加速方法往往以牺牲质量为代价换取速度，亟需一种既能加速又能保持（甚至提升）质量的方法。

Method: Archer方法的核心思想是"非对称缓存"：将可变的响应状态与当前假设保持同步（不缓存），而在有界状态邻域内重复使用提示词的K/V缓存。由于提示词的token身份在双向注意力下保持不变，有界复用可以分摊重复的提示词计算成本。该方法还延迟了暂定token的反馈，避免过早强化瞬态高置信度错误，从而给回滚更多纠错机会。理论分析将提示词复用刻画为与可逆性对齐的缓存边界，界定了状态相关近似误差，并给出了保持全刷新决策的解码器边界条件。

Result: Arc法在主测试套件上实现了33.63%的平均最佳性能和2.57倍平均加速，在所有评估设置中Pass@1提升最高达3.05个百分点，加速最高达2.95倍。受控分析验证了质量提升来源于延迟提示反馈和状态感知刷新机制。

Conclusion: Archer通过巧妙的非对称KV缓存设计，打破了DLM加速与质量之间的权衡，不仅加速推理还能提升生成质量。该方法为回滚型扩散语言模型的高效推理开辟了新途径，表明提示词状态的可复用性可以转化为计算效率同时改善生成动态。

Abstract: Diffusion language models (DLMs) iteratively refine a sequence, allowing earlier predictions to be revised as context evolves. This rollback capability distinguishes them from irreversible autoregressive generation, but makes inference costly. Every denoising update alters the global context, forcing both prompt and response states to be recomputed even though only response tokens are revisable. Key-value (KV) caching could reduce this cost, yet conventional caching assumes immutable historical states and is therefore difficult to reconcile with rollback.In this paper, we introduce Adaptive Reuse of Cached Hidden States for Efficient Rollback (Archer), a training-free KV caching method for rollback-capable DLMs. Archer asymmetrically keeps the mutable response synchronized with the current hypothesis while reusing prompt K/V within a bounded state neighborhood. Although prompt representations also change under bidirectional attention, their token identities remain fixed; bounded reuse therefore amortizes repeated prompt computation without caching mutable response states. It also delays feedback from tentative tokens, reducing premature reinforcement of transient high-confidence errors and giving rollback more opportunity to correct them. Our analysis characterizes prompt reuse as a reversibility-aligned cache boundary, bounds its state-dependent approximation error, and gives a decoder-margin condition for preserving full-refresh decisions.Existing DLM acceleration often trades quality for speed. Archer shifts this frontier, attaining the best mean performance of 33.63% together with a 2.57x mean speedup on the main suite. Across evaluated settings, it improves Pass@1 by up to 3.05 points and reaches up to 2.95x speedup. Controlled analyses connect the quality gain to delayed prompt feedback and validate state-aware refresh. Our code is available at https://github.com/Hxnng/Archer.

</details>


### [47] [Wisdom in Unity: The Role of Multilingual Training in Figurative Language Identification in Proverbs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08090)
*Rama Alomair,Remas Alsubaie,Walaa Saifalislam,Rima Alsonbul,Mona Alnajjar,Razan Aldossari,Haya Alibrahim,Abeer Aldayel*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了多语言监督对习语/谚语识别任务的影响，使用7种语言、6,787个翻译实例和742个谚语概念，提出了一个四维注释框架（隐喻、道德/劝诫、因果关系、文化特定），发现约50%的翻译训练数据即可达到接近最优性能，并揭示了文化特定形式在多语言监督下收益最大。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管多语言比喻识别并非新课题，但超越语言同质训练数据的转变需要对翻译多语言监督的贡献有更清晰的理解。作者旨在系统性地探究翻译后的多语言监督数据在多样化的修辞形式下如何影响比喻语言识别性能。

Method: 使用742个谚语概念、6,787个翻译实例（涵盖7种语言），评估5种模型（包括多语言编码器和指令微调的大语言模型），在逐步增加多语言监督水平的条件下进行测试。同时提出一个多维注释框架，从四个互补的修辞形式（隐喻、道德/劝诫、因果关系、文化特定）对谚语进行刻画。

Result: （1）约50%的翻译多语言训练数据即可达到接近最优的比喻语言识别性能；（2）组合多样化的修辞形式带来最强的整体性能；（3）最少出现的文化特定形式在多语言监督下获得最大的性能提升；（4）道德/劝诫和文化特定形式对指令微调LLM的比喻识别贡献最大。

Conclusion: 研究发现促使多语言比喻语言识别应从以隐喻为中心的分类法转向概念级别的多维框架，显式地对互补形式的比喻意义进行建模。

Abstract: Although multilingual approaches to figurative language identification are not new, the shift beyond language homogeneous training data requires a clearer understanding of the contribution of translated multilingual supervision. We examine this question using 742 proverb concepts across 6,787 translated instances in seven languages. We evaluate five models, including multilingual encoders and instruction tuned LLMs, under progressively increasing levels of multilingual supervision. Moreover, we introduce a multidimensional annotation framework for proverbs that characterizes them through four complementary figurative forms: Metaphorical, Moral/Advisory, Cause-Effect, and Culture Specific.
  Our findings show that approximately 50% of the translated multilingual training data is sufficient to achieve near-optimal figurative language identification performance. We further show that combining diverse figurative forms yields the strongest overall performance. A notable finding is that the least frequent figurative form, Culture Specific, exhibits the largest performance gains under multilingual supervision. Furthermore, the Moral/Advisory and Culture Specific forms contribute most to the performance of instruction-tuned LLMs on figurative language identification. These findings motivate multilingual figurative language identification to move beyond metaphor-centric taxonomies toward concept level multidimensional frameworks that explicitly model complementary forms of figurative meaning.

</details>


### [48] [NeuPAT: Neuron-aware Plasticity Allocation Tuning for Language-Preserving MLLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08107)
*Jiayue Jin,Jingwei Zhang,Chen Wang,Jing Liu,Longteng Guo*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出NeuPAT框架，通过神经元级别的可塑性感知分配策略，在多模态指令微调中保护对语言能力至关重要的神经元，从而减少多模态扩展对LLM语言智能的损害。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态扩展LLM虽然赋予了新的感知能力，但通常会损害预训练阶段获得的语言智能。已有研究缺乏对内部适应动力学的深入理解，需要从神经元层面的可塑性差异出发，探索如何在保持多模态能力的同时减少语言能力的退化。

Method: NeuPAT框架包含两个关键设计：(1) 通过小规模探测阶段估计预训练LLM中不同神经元在多模态学习过程中的适应模式；(2) 在微调过程中分配神经元级别的更新约束——对语言敏感神经元施加保护（减少更新），同时允许更具可塑性的神经元充分参与多模态知识的适应。该方法轻量级且架构无关。

Result: 在多个LLM家族上的实验表明，NeuPAT在11个语言基准测试上恢复了原始vanilla多模态微调导致的94.5%语言能力退化，同时保持了相当的多模态任务性能。

Conclusion: NeuPAT提供了一种有效的能力保持型多模态扩展方法，验证了神经元异质可塑性假说的实用性，为多模态LLM的可持续发展提供了新思路。

Abstract: Multimodal expansion of large language models (LLMs) enables new perceptual capabilities but often compromises the language intelligence acquired during pretraining. In this work, we investigate this phenomenon from the perspective of internal adaptation dynamics and discover that neurons in pretrained LLMs exhibit heterogeneous plasticity during multimodal learning: some neurons are critical for preserving language capabilities, while others are more adaptive to multimodal knowledge. Based on this insight, we propose NeuPAT (Neuron-aware Plasticity Allocation Tuning), a lightweight and architecture-agnostic framework that allocates neuron-wise update constraints during multimodal instruction tuning. NeuPAT uses a small-scale probing stage to estimate neuron adaptation patterns and selectively protects language-sensitive neurons while promoting multimodal adaptation through more plastic neurons. Experiments across diverse LLM families demonstrate that NeuPAT recovers 94.5\% of the language capability degradation caused by vanilla tuning on 11 language benchmarks while maintaining comparable multimodal performance, providing an effective approach for capability-preserving multimodal expansion.

</details>


### [49] [Can LLM Agents Stick to the Script? A Benchmark for Long-Horizon Consistency in Interactive Narratives](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08160)
*Yingpeng Ma,Jianhao Yan,Bei Shi,Ka Hou Kam,Runnan Wang,Xuebo Liu,Yulong Chen,Yue Zhang,Derek F. Wong*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出NCP-Bench基准，用于评估LLM在交互式叙事中维持长期逻辑一致性和叙事承诺的能力，实验表明即便是GPT-5.2等最强模型在对抗性干预下也仅达到42%的20轮存活率，揭示了大语言模型在长视界叙事一致性方面的显著不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM驱动的AI游戏与交互式叙事研究多关注生成流畅度和开放性，却忽视了用户在不受约束地干预时叙事长期逻辑一致性与完整性的保持问题，这一关键挑战尚无系统性评估框架。

Method: 将挑战形式化为Narrative Commitment Preservation (NCP)问题，基于电影剧情摘要构建100个叙事环境的NCP-Bench基准；每个环境包含结构化叙事规范（轨迹、承诺及初始事实），可在玩家代理与叙述者代理交互过程中自动检查。

Result: 对最先进LLM的实验显示长视界一致性存在显著差距：最佳模型GPT-5.2在20轮后存活率仅42%，各模型事实冲突率在40%-68%之间，且在100轮限制内仅个别运行能满足所有成就承诺。

Conclusion: 高语言质量并不保证承诺保持；即使强模型也容易被对抗性干预诱导生成逻辑冲突内容，表明NCP是LLM驱动交互叙事中亟待解决的关键问题。

Abstract: The rapid advancement of Large Language Models (LLMs) is revolutionizing AI for Games by enabling open-ended and fluid interactive storytelling. However, existing research has largely overlooked the critical challenge of maintaining long-horizon logical consistency and narrative integrity against unconstrained user interventions. To address this, we formulate this challenge as Narrative Commitment Preservation (NCP), and take interactive narrative as our testbed. We introduce NCP-Bench, a benchmark of 100 narrative environments derived from movie synopses. Each environment includes a structured narrative specification (trajectory, commitments, and initial facts) that we can automatically check throughout the interaction between the player agent and the narrator agent. Experiments across state-of-the-art LLMs reveal a substantial long-horizon consistency gap: high linguistic quality does not guarantee commitment preservation; even strong models frequently generate logically conflicting content under adversarial interventions, with the best-performing model (GPT-5.2) achieving only 42% survival rate after 20 turns and fact conflict rates ranging from 40% to 68% across models, and only isolated runs satisfying all achievement commitments within the 100-turn limit.

</details>

### [50] [Differentiate the Solver, Not the Equation: Reverse-Sweep Adjoints for Block Implicit Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08559)
*Lei Shu,Ying Jiang,Kui Wu,Yin Yang,Leonidas Guibas,Chenfanfu Jiang*

Main category: cs.GR

TL;DR: 本文提出"求解器级微分"（solver-level differentiation）方法，通过对实际执行的求解器本身进行反向微分（而非对收敛方程或展开的计算图求导），在保证与自动微分精确一致的前提下，大幅降低内存和计算开销。以Vertex Block Descent求解器为例，反向反色Gauss-Seidel扫描仅由局部3×3伴随求解组成，相比展开式自动微分快33倍、内存少71倍，并可将可微弹性动力学扩展到单GPU上100万个接触耦合软体（800万顶点）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有可微模拟方法存在两难困境：展开式自动微分（unrolled AD）内存随求解器深度线性增长；方程级隐式微分（equation-level）需要组装全局雅可比并求解大规模稀疏伴随系统，丢弃了前向求解器的局部性，且微分的是收敛方程而非实际运行的有穷计算。作者希望设计一种兼顾两者优点的方法——对实际执行的求解器本身进行微分，保留前向求解器的局部性和稀疏结构。

Method: 核心思想是将求解器视为由块隐式更新（block implicit updates）组成的算子序列。前向过程中，求解器通过有序的局部求解实现一个近似逆；反向过程则按相反顺序应用相应的伴随更新，形成反向扫描（reverse-sweep）公式，其反向传播完全镜像前向求解器。作者将其实例化在Vertex Block Descent上，得到反向反色Gauss-Seidel扫描，仅由局部3×3伴随求解组成，不构造任何全局系统。

Result: 1）在各种求解器深度下，求解器级微分与自动微分在机器精度级别完全一致，而方程级伴随在单次扫描后误差高达37%；2）在同一代码库、同一GPU的受控比较中，比展开式自动微分快33倍、内存占用少71倍；3）该方法同样精确适用于投影动力学（projective dynamics）和扩展位置动力学（extended position-based dynamics）；4）在单GPU上将可微弹性动力学扩展到100万个接触耦合软体（800万顶点）。

Conclusion: 本文证明求解器结构本身可以作为一种实用的组织原则来设计高效的可微模拟算法。通过微分实际执行的求解器而非收敛方程或展开计算图，既获得了自动微分的精确性，又获得了方程级方法的效率优势，为大规模可微物理模拟提供了新的范式。

Abstract: Differentiable simulation is a key component in learning, control, and inverse problems, where gradients through nonlinear implicit solvers are required. Existing approaches either rely on unrolled automatic differentiation, whose memory grows with solver depth, or on equation-level implicit differentiation, which assembles global Jacobians and solves large sparse adjoint systems, discarding the locality of the forward solver -- and differentiating the converged equation rather than the finite computation that actually ran. We propose solver-level differentiation, which differentiates the executed solver itself. When a solver is composed of block implicit updates, its discrete adjoint is obtained by applying the corresponding adjoint updates in reverse order, yielding a reverse-sweep formulation whose backward pass mirrors the forward solver. From an operator perspective, the forward pass realizes an approximate inverse through ordered local solves, and the backward applies its transpose through reverse local adjoint solves, constructing no global system. We instantiate this idea on Vertex Block Descent, yielding a differentiable solver whose reverse colored Gauss-Seidel sweeps are composed entirely of local $3\times 3$ adjoint solves. The backward matches automatic differentiation through the identical executed forward to machine precision at every solver depth, where the equation-level adjoint is off by 37% after one sweep; in a controlled same-codebase, same-GPU comparison it is 33x faster and uses 71x less memory than unrolled automatic differentiation; and the same construction is exact on projective dynamics and extended position-based dynamics. We scale differentiable elastodynamics to $10^6$ contact-coupled soft bodies (8M vertices) on one GPU. Overall, this work highlights solver structure as a practical organizing principle for efficient differentiable simulation.

</details>


### [51] [Adaptive Volumetric Parameterization of Simply Connected 3-Manifolds with Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08672)
*Zhiyuan Lyu,Gary P. T. Choi*

Main category: cs.GR

TL;DR: 提出了一种针对单连通三维流形的体参数化框架，通过联合控制局部形状与质量畸变，并在优化过程中自适应调整目标域，支持从固体椭球到自由边界域的三种渐进灵活的目标域设置。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大多数现有体参数化方法仅使用标准域（如实心球）作为目标域，而不考虑给定三维流形的整体形状，导致显著的几何畸变，影响后续的形状处理与分析任务。

Method: 框架包含三个核心组件：3D拟共形形状更新、基于扩散的密度均衡更新，以及去除单元折叠的几何校正过程。支持三种目标域设置：固定固体椭球、变半径的体积归一化自适应椭球、以及海平面嵌入的自由边界域。

Result: 实验结果表明该框架在体参数化中有效控制了形状与质量畸变，并可轻松应用于多分辨率与局部自适应体网格重划分、体配准和体变形等任务。

Conclusion: 该工作为三维流形的表示、处理与分析提供了一种新途径，通过自适应目标域策略克服了传统标准域参数化的畸变瓶颈。

Abstract: Volumetric parameterization, the process of mapping a 3-manifold onto a simplified volumetric domain, is important for many tasks in computer graphics and imaging science. However, most prior volumetric parameterization approaches have only utilized standardized domains such as a solid ball regardless of the overall shape of the given 3-manifolds, which introduces significant geometric distortion and affects the subsequent shape processing and analysis tasks. To overcome this issue, in this work we propose a novel volumetric parameterization framework for simply connected 3-manifolds. Specifically, the proposed framework jointly controls local shape and mass distortions, while adapting the target domain during the optimization process. It enables three progressively more flexible target-domain settings for the parameterization: a prescribed solid ellipsoid, a volume-normalized adaptive ellipsoid with variable radii, and a sea-embedded free-boundary domain. For each setting, the parameterization algorithm consists of a 3D quasi-conformality shape update, a diffusion-based density-equalizing update, and a geometric correction procedure for removing element foldings, thereby allowing for volumetric parameterizations with different desired effects. Experimental results are presented to demonstrate the effectiveness of our proposed framework. Moreover, our framework can be easily applied to multiresolution and localized adaptive volumetric remeshing, volumetric registration, and volumetric morphing. Altogether, our work provides a new way for the representation, processing, and analysis of 3-manifolds.

</details>


### [52] [Smooth Motion Stitching via Laplacian Optimization in Rodrigues Vector Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08986)
*Ryosuke Higasayama,Hideki Todo,Jongseong Gwak*

Main category: cs.GR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于Rodrigues向量空间中拉普拉斯优化的运动编辑框架，用于平滑运动拼接。通过将关节旋转表示为连续Rodrigues向量，将运动拼接建模为时间拉普拉斯优化问题，实现运动片段间的平滑过渡，同时保留参考运动的特征时间变化。该方法无需学习模型或复杂手工调参，计算高效，支持类内替换和跨类别运动拼接。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的运动拼接方法在过渡平滑性和计算效率方面存在不足，且往往依赖学习模型或复杂的手工参数调整。此外，旋转表示的选择对时间优化的数值稳定性有重要影响，而此前缺少对旋转连续性这一关键因素的系统分析与解释。

Method: 将关节旋转表示为连续Rodrigues向量，将运动拼接问题形式化为时间拉普拉斯优化问题，在优化过程中保持参考运动的特征时间变化；同时从理论上分析了旋转连续性，解释旋转轴翻转在实际运动数据中极少出现，从而避免了合成轴翻转场景下的数值不稳定问题。

Result: 通过一系列拼接实验及与线性插值的对比，拉普拉斯编辑在广泛的运动差异条件下产生了稳定且视觉上连贯的过渡。旋转连续性分析证实了实际运动数据中旋转轴反转罕见，解释了合成轴翻转场景中数值不稳定的现象为何不会在实际运动拼接中出现。

Conclusion: 该框架强调了旋转表示对时间优化稳定性的重要意义，不仅适用于动画创作，还可扩展至运动分析以及融合感知或生理线索的未来研究。

Abstract: This paper presents a motion editing framework for smooth motion stitching based on Laplacian optimization in Rodrigues vector space. By representing joint rotations as continuous Rodrigues vectors, motion stitching is formulated as a temporal Laplacian optimization problem, enabling smooth transitions between motion segments while preserving characteristic temporal variations of reference motions. The proposed approach supports both intra-category replacement and cross-category motion stitching without relying on learning-based models or complex manual tuning, and is computationally efficient for interactive editing. Through a series of stitching experiments and comparisons with linear interpolation, we demonstrate that Laplacian editing produces stable and visually coherent transitions under a wide range of motion differences. Furthermore, an analysis of rotational continuity clarifies that rotation-axis inversions are rare in real motion data and explains why numerical instabilities observed in synthetic axis-flipping scenarios do not arise in practical motion stitching. These results highlight the importance of rotational representation in stabilizing temporal optimization and suggest that the proposed framework is well suited not only for animation authoring but also for motion analysis and future extensions incorporating perceptual or physiological cues.

</details>


### [53] [A Hybrid Neural-Microfacet BRDF Model for Real-Time Rendering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09604)
*Louis De Oliveira,Anastasia Karpova,Georges Nader,Antoine Houdard,Pierre Mezieres,Damien Rioux-Lavoie,Romain Pacanowski*

Main category: cs.GR

TL;DR: 本文提出一种混合BRDF模型，将GGX型微表面模型与神经网络模型结合，利用神经组件校正微表面组件的近似误差，以更小的网络尺寸实现更高的测量数据拟合精度，同时保持艺术家的可编辑性和重要性采样能力，适用于实时渲染。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 微表面BRDF模型虽然在实时渲染中广泛使用，但难以再现复杂光-表面交互产生的细微外观效果；而专门化的物理模型虽更精确但缺乏通用性和实时性能；现有神经模型需要较大的网络，实时渲染成本过高。

Method: 提出混合模型架构：结合GGX型微表面模型与神经模型。神经组件仅用于校正微表面组件的近似外观误差，而非全量建模BRDF，从而大幅缩小所需网络规模。系统还设计了可编辑性与重要性采样方案。

Result: 在相同内存成本下，该混合模型比现有的最先进神经模型更好地逼近测量数据，计算开销仅略高于纯微表面模型；同时保持艺术家可编辑性并支持重要性采样。

Conclusion: 混合微表面+神经的BRDF表示在精度、性能、可编辑性和采样效率之间取得了良好平衡，使其同时适用于离线渲染和实时渲染场景。

Abstract: Over the past decade, microfacet-based BRDF models have formed the foundation of real-time rendering pipelines. Despite their widespread use, they often fail to reproduce subtle appearance effects arising from complex light-surface interactions, which have led to the emergence of specialized physics-based models for specific optical phenomena (e.g., diffraction, iridescence, multilayers). Although more accurate, these models lose versatility and lack performance for real-time rendering. Recently introduced, neural models have demonstrated their ability to approximate BRDF reference data coming from measurements, simulations, or even complex shading networks. However, most current neural models require relatively large networks, making them costly for real-time rendering. In this paper, we introduce a hybrid model that combines a GGX-type microfacet model and a neural model to leverage the best features of both representations. The neural component corrects the appearance approximated by the microfacet component, allowing much smaller network than in existing neural models. We show that, at identical memory cost, our model approximates measurements better than state-of-the-art neural models for a low evaluation overhead compared to a microfacet-based model. Furthermore, our hybrid model remains easily editable by artists and benefits from an important sampling scheme, making it attractive for both offline and real-time rendering.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

### [54] [Application of Artificial Intelligence for Fraudulent Banking Operations Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07471)
*Bohdan Mytnyk,Oleksandr Tkachyk,Nataliya Shakhovska,Solomiia Fedushko,Yuriy Syerov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究利用机器学习算法识别银行欺诈交易，重点开发了人工神经网络模型，并通过数据预处理、特征工程和类别不平衡处理等技术提升检测精度。实验结果显示逻辑回归AUC约为0.946，而堆叠泛化（Stacked Generalization）方法达到更优的0.954。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: COVID-19疫情期间，大量银行业务转向线上平台，同时不法分子利用慈善基金等名义进行网络诈骗，导致银行欺诈事件显著增加。因此，迫切需要开发有效的智能识别模型来检测欺诈性银行交易，以保障数字社会中用户的资金安全。

Method: 本文采用多种机器学习算法进行欺诈交易识别，包括逻辑回归和人工神经网络等。研究的创新点在于：1）开发用于识别欺诈交易的机器学习模型；2）设计银行数据预处理技术；3）采用类别不平衡处理、特征变换和特征工程等方法提升检测精度；4）构建堆叠泛化集成模型进行预测。

Result: 实验结果表明，在所有单一算法中，逻辑回归表现最佳，AUC值约为0.946；而堆叠泛化（Stacked Generalization）集成方法进一步提升了性能，AUC达到0.954。不同算法的结果均通过可视化方式呈现。

Conclusion: 基于人工智能算法的银行欺诈识别是当前数字社会中的热点问题。堆叠泛化方法相比单一模型具有更优的欺诈检测性能，本研究为在线银行交易欺诈检测提供了有效的解决方案。

Abstract: This study considers the task of applying artificial intelligence to recognize bank fraud. In recent years, due to the COVID19 pandemic, bank fraud has become even more common due to the massive transition of many operations to online platforms and the creation of many charitable funds that criminals can use to deceive users. The present work focuses on machine learning algorithms as a tool well suited for analyzing and recognizing online banking transactions. The study`s scientific novelty is the development of machine learning models for identifying fraudulent banking transactions and techniques for preprocessing bank data for further comparison and selection of the best results. This paper also details various methods for improving detection accuracy, i.e., handling highly imbalanced datasets, feature transformation, and feature engineering. The proposed model, which is based on an artificial neural network, effectively improves the accuracy of fraudulent transaction detection. The results of the different algorithms are visualized, and the logistic regression algorithm performs the best, with an output AUC value of approximately 0,946. The stacked generalization shows a better AUC of 0.954. The recognition of banking fraud using artificial intelligence algorithms is a topical issue in our digital society.

</details>


### [55] [Data-Driven Fire-Zone Segmentation for Improved Short-Term Wildfire Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07472)
*Nicolas Caron,Christophe Guyeux,Hassan Noura,Benjamin Aynes*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文挑战了野火预测中传统的均匀网格划分范式，提出了一种无监督的火灾区域分割算法（结合分水岭检测与K-means聚类），直接基于历史火灾模式定义预测单元。在法国六个省和六种预测模型上的实验表明，该方法相比网格法在IoU上提升3-6%，且计算轻量（每次配置<10秒）、完全可并行化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有野火预测模型通常将研究区域离散化为均匀网格，忽视了火灾点燃点在空间上的异质性分布。作者旨在证明数据离散化方式对预测性能的影响比模型选择更重要，从而挑战现有范式。

Method: 提出无监督火灾区域分割算法：(1) 使用分水岭检测（watershed detection）识别火灾空间模式；(2) 结合K-means聚类将历史火灾模式定义为预测单元（fire zones），替代传统的均匀网格作为模型输入。

Result: 在法国六个省、六种预测模型上的实验显示，火灾区域分割法始终优于基于网格的方法，平均IoU提升3-6%，且效果依赖于空间尺度；方法计算开销低（每次配置<10秒），可完全并行化。

Conclusion: 优化空间离散化策略能够为短期野火预测带来显著且可复现的性能增益，表明数据分区的设计选择在预测建模中可能比模型架构本身更为关键。

Abstract: Wildfire prediction models typically discretize study areas into uniform grids, ignoring the heterogeneous spatial distribution of ignitions. We challenge this paradigm by showing that how data is discretized matters more than which model is used. We propose an unsupervised fire-zone segmentation algorithm combining watershed detection with K-means clustering to define prediction units directly from historical fire patterns. Experiments across six French departments and six forecasting models show that fire-zone segmentation consistently outperforms grid-based approaches, with mean IoU improvements of +3--6% depending on spatial scale. The method is computationally lightweight (<10s per configuration) and fully parallelizable. Our results demonstrate that optimizing spatial discretization yields significant, reproducible performance gains for short-term wildfire forecasting.

</details>


### [56] [Evolving Safety Landscape of Multi-modal Large Language Models: A Survey of Emerging Threats and Safeguards](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07535)
*Xi Li,Shu Zhao,Xiaohan Zou,Fei Zhao,Fuxiao Liu,Yusen Zhang,Cheng Han,Yushun Dong,Jiaqi Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统地调研了多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）不断演变的安全格局，提出了一个多模态grounded的安全威胁分类体系，分析了威胁模型的转变，并整理了现有的安全策略与未来方向。

  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 该调查首先提出了一个多模态grounded的安全威胁分类体系，分析威胁模型的转变，覆盖对抗攻击、数据投毒、越狱和幻觉等威胁类型；随后总结更新后的安全假设，并据此组织梳理近期MLLM安全策略的研究进展；最后讨论开放挑战和未来方向。

Result: 提出了一套系统化的MLLM安全威胁分类框架，明确了威胁模型从单模态向多模态转变的核心特征，并基于新的安全假设整合了现有防御策略。

Conclusion: MLLM的安全问题需要在多模态grounded框架下重新审视，未来需要开发更principled和可扩展的安全机制以应对多模态系统的独特挑战。

Abstract: Multi-modal large language models (MLLMs) integrate heterogeneous modalities through modality alignment and fusion, enabling stronger understanding and reasoning. However, this architectural shift reshapes the safety landscape of machine learning. Increased model complexity and cross-modal interactions give rise to novel threats, including compromised modality integration, modality misalignment, and fused safety risks, reflecting shifts in threat modeling beyond uni-modal assumptions. These shifts, in turn, impose new constraints on safety solutions not captured by existing frameworks rooted in uni-modal learning. Motivated by these challenges, this survey provides a systematic analysis of the evolving safety landscape of MLLMs. We first propose a multimodal grounded taxonomy of safety threats and analyze shifts in threat models, covering adversarial attacks, data poisoning, jailbreaks, and hallucinations. We then summarize updated safety assumptions and organize recent advances in MLLM safety strategies accordingly. Finally, we discuss open challenges and future directions to inform the development of more principled and scalable safety mechanisms for multimodal systems.

</details>


### [57] [Tracing sources of epistemic uncertainty in deep learning predictions: homo- and hetero-scedastic linearized estimators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07630)
*Pierre Nodet,Thomas George*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了将两种经典统计估计器适配到现代深度学习框架中，用于量化并区分两类不确定性来源：偶然不确定性（aleatoric uncertainty）和数据稀缺导致的不确定性（epistemic uncertainty）。通过利用近似Fisher信息矩阵的近期进展，该方法可扩展到实际的深度学习架构上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习中不确定性量化对于可靠决策至关重要，但现有方法难以清晰地区分不确定性来自于数据的固有噪声（偶然不确定性）还是局部数据稀缺（认识不确定性）。研究者希望借助经典统计工具为这一区分提供更清晰的洞察。

Method: 将两种经典统计估计器（两种估计器用于分别量化偶然不确定性和数据稀缺引起的不确定性）适配到深度学习框架中，利用近似Fisher信息矩阵的最新进展，使估计器能够扩展到真实的深度学习架构规模。

Result: 实验结果表明，每个测试点受到两类不确定性来源的影响程度不同，这验证了估计器的实用性，并展示了它们在提升真实世界应用鲁棒性方面的价值。

Conclusion: 所提出的估计器能够有效区分偶然不确定性与数据稀缺导致的不确定性，为实际应用中改善模型鲁棒性提供了实用工具。

Abstract: We adapt two classical statistical estimators for quantifying uncertainty to modern deep learning, in order to provide clearer insights into uncertainty attributable to two sources : aleatoric uncertainty, or locally scarce data. Our approach leverages recent advances in approximate Fisher Information Matrices, to enable scaling to actual architectures. Experimental results demonstrate how each test points is differentially impacted by both sources, highlighting the practical utility of our estimators in improving the robustness of real-world applications.

</details>


### [58] [SkillConsist: Detecting Inconsistencies in Agent Skills via Bidirectional Graph Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07639)
*Chaofan Meng,Yuhang Zheng,Yingnan Zhou,Sihan Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出SkillConsist，一种用于检测LLM Agent Skill声明与实现之间不一致性的方法。它通过LLM将声明和实现内容分离为行为记录并构建行为图，再利用双向图对齐和图差分识别冲突，在633个Skill基准上显著超越现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Agent Skill的不一致性可能暴露未声明的危险行为或导致错误的Skill选择。现有的检测方法要么依赖预定义类别或声明范围来评估行为/安全属性图，要么如PL-HCL使用LLM学习元数据、指令和资源间的一致性，但无法有效处理声明与实现行为在文本和代码中混杂、以及简洁声明对应多个连接实现步骤的挑战。

Method: SkillConsist包含三个关键步骤：(1) 使用LLM将声明和实现内容分别分离为行为记录，并辅以静态分析补充实现侧记录；(2) 在声明侧和实现侧分别构建行为图，从任意一侧的行为记录出发执行双向图对齐，在另一侧搜索候选子图并沿行为关系扩展直到完整表达源侧行为；(3) 通过图差分识别对齐子图之间的冲突并输出检测结果。

Result: 在由ClawHub上500个最常下载的公开Skills和133个Skill-Inject包组成的633个Skill基准上（含319个不一致、314个一致的Skill及442个局部不一致标注），SkillConsist在包级检测上达到86.85%精确率、89.03%召回率和87.93% F1，较最佳基线F1提升20.43个百分点；在局部定位上达到67.60%精确率、58.14%召回率和62.52% F1。

Conclusion: SkillConsist通过行为图双向对齐与图差分的创新方法，有效解决了声明与实现行为在文本/代码间混杂及复杂多步实现关联的检测难题，在包级检测和局部定位上均达到领先水平，为Agent Skill一致性检测提供了更可靠的方案。

Abstract: Agent Skills provide reusable capabilities to LLM agents. Agent Skill inconsistencies can expose undisclosed dangerous behavior or cause wrong Skill selection. Recent Agent Skill research has increasingly examined Agent Skill consistency detection. Existing methods evaluate behaviors or security-property graphs against predefined categories or declared scopes. More recently, PL-HCL uses an LLM-based model to learn consistency across metadata, instructions, and resources. However, declaration and implementation behavior can be mixed across text and code, and a concise declaration can correspond to multiple connected implementation steps. We present SkillConsist to address both challenges. An LLM separates declaration and implementation content into behavior records on the implementation and declaration sides, while static analysis supplements implementation records. These records form declaration and implementation behavior graphs, respectively. Starting from a behavior record on either side, bidirectional graph alignment searches the other graph for a candidate subgraph and expands it along behavior relations until it completely expresses the source-side behavior. Graph differencing identifies conflicts between aligned subgraphs and outputs the detection results. We construct a 633-Skill benchmark from ClawHub's 500 most-downloaded public Skills and 133 Skill-Inject packages. The benchmark contains 319 inconsistent and 314 consistent Skills and 442 localized inconsistency annotations. On this benchmark, SkillConsist achieves 86.85% precision, 89.03% recall, and 87.93% F1 for package-level detection, improving F1 over the best baseline by 20.43 percentage points. For localization, it achieves 67.60% precision, 58.14% recall, and 62.52% F1.

</details>


### [59] [PhysAttNet: Enhancing Predictive Performance in Industrial and Astrophysical Time Series via Physics-Informed Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07681)
*Amal Saadallah,Julia Tjus,Petra Wiederkeher,Wolfgang Rhode*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出PhysAttNet，一种物理信息约束的注意力框架，用于时间序列预测。通过在轻量级CNN预测器上引入三种正则化约束（对齐、平滑性、稀疏性），使注意力机制遵循物理信号的结构特性，在铣削切削力预测和耀变体耀斑预测两个应用中提升了预测精度、泛化能力和重要事件的预测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CNN在时间序列预测中虽高效且表征能力强，但其学习到的时序注意力模式往往不稳定或物理上不一致，导致鲁棒性、泛化性和可解释性下降，尤其在物理过程中需要捕捉局部结构对应物理事件的场景下问题突出。

Method: PhysAttNet在轻量级CNN预测器上附加一个受领域信息正则化引导的注意力头。训练中施加三个互补约束：对齐正则化（促使注意力遵循输入信号平滑、峰值中心的时序结构）、平滑性正则化（强制时序连续演化）、稀疏性正则化（选择性地聚焦信息丰富的区间）。这些可微正则项引入物理引导的归纳偏置，无需标注解释或人工监督。

Result: 在铣削切削力预测和耀变体耀斑时间序列预测两个不同应用上，PhysAttNet相比基线提升了预测精度、泛化能力，并对结构上重要事件的预测性能有显著改善。

Conclusion: 物理引导的正则化约束能够有效改善CNN在时间序列预测中的注意力稳定性与可靠性，在不依赖人工标注的情况下增强模型的鲁棒性和可解释性，验证了物理知识融入深度学习模型的实用价值。

Abstract: Accurate and robust time series forecasting is essential in many applications involving physical processes, such as manufacturing monitoring and astrophysical event detection. In these settings, predictive models must remain reliable under noise, variability, and measurement uncertainty while capturing temporally localized structures corresponding to physically meaningful events. Convolutional neural networks (CNNs) are widely used for such tasks due to their computational efficiency and strong representational capacity. However, their learned temporal representations often exhibit unstable or physically inconsistent attention patterns, reducing robustness, generalization, and interpretability. This paper introduces PhysAttNet, a physics-informed attention framework for time series forecasting. PhysAttNet augments a lightweight CNN forecaster with an attention head guided by domain-informed regularization reflecting the structural properties of physical signals. Specifically, three complementary constraints are imposed during training: an alignment regularization that encourages attention to follow smooth, peak-centered temporal structures derived from the input signal, a smoothness regularization that enforces continuous temporal evolution, and a sparsity regularization that promotes selective focus on informative intervals. These differentiable regularization terms introduce physics-guided inductive bias without requiring annotated explanations or manual supervision. Experiments on two distinct applications, namely predicting cutting forces during milling and forecasting flares in blazar time series, demonstrate that PhysAttNet improves forecasting accuracy, generalization, and prediction performance on structurally important events.

</details>


### [60] [CODS: Iterative Bellman-Residual Data Selection for Reusable Offline Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07719)
*Ibne Farabi Shihab,Sanjeda Akter,Abu Sa-Adat Mohamed Moon-Im Al Ahsan,Md Najmus Swaqeeb,Anuj Sharma*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CODS（Critic-guided Data Selection）是一种批评者引导的离线强化学习数据选择方法，通过交替进行算法匹配的批评者拟合与高残差转移获取，冻结可复用子集，在10%预算下保留约96%的全量池性能，超越多种现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离线强化学习从固定转移池中反复训练策略，跨种子和超参数时冗余数据成本高昂；而朴素的子采样可能移除长时程信用分配所需的稀有转移。现有方法如优先回放产生的是动态结构而非静态产物，一次性残差选择则无法随批评者更新而刷新选择评分。

Method: CODS采用交替迭代机制：先拟合与目标算法匹配的批评者（critic），然后获取高残差（high-residual）转移，如此反复后冻结一个可复用的数据子集。与优先回放不同，CODS产出静态数据产物；与一次性残差选择不同，它会随批评者变化刷新评分。

Result: 在10%预算下，CODS在20个有效的D4RL任务-算法单元上保留了96.6%的全量池性能；在19/20个单元上超越ReDOR和OPER，在20/20个单元上超越所有其他子集基线。六个子集优势在预声明层次推断与Holm校正下仍然显著。固定总选择器更新次数时，五轮获取比一轮平均提升11.23点并在之后饱和。等轮次和等时间评估表明复用而非单次加速带来计算优势。全轨迹扩展在ALFWorld保留95.4%成功率，在GSM8K保留96.5%精确匹配。

Conclusion: CODS是一种可复用的数据选择流程而非正式的coreset保证，其机制与腐败干预实验揭示了它对稀疏奖励富集的有效性以及对离群值的敏感性。

Abstract: Offline reinforcement learning repeatedly trains policies from a fixed transition pool, making redundant data costly across seeds and hyperparameters, while naive subsampling can remove rare transitions needed for long-horizon credit assignment. We introduce CODS, a critic-guided selector that alternates between fitting an algorithm-matched critic and acquiring high-residual transitions before freezing a reusable subset. Unlike prioritized replay, CODS produces a static artifact; unlike one-shot residual selection, it refreshes scores as the critic changes. At a 10\% budget, CODS retains 96.6\% of eligible-pool performance across 20 valid D4RL task--algorithm cells. It exceeds ReDOR and OPER on 19/20 cells and every other subset baseline on 20/20; all six subset advantages remain significant under predeclared hierarchical inference with Holm correction. Holding total selector updates fixed, five acquisition rounds improve four representative cells by 11.23 points over one round and saturate thereafter. Equal-pass and equal-hour evaluations clarify that reuse, rather than a single-run speedup, creates the compute advantage. Mechanism and corruption interventions expose both useful sparse-reward enrichment and sensitivity to outliers.
