TL;DR: Agent-MD是一种将大语言模型推理选择性嵌入长时程分子模拟工作流的框架：常规模拟、分析、续算和存档由基于规则持久代理处理，LLM仅在事件触发时介入审查。在GCMC-MD水蒸气脱附战役中，该框架成功完成120段模拟循环，且无需实时推理调用，同时通过盲测推理回放识别了工作流问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时程分子模拟需要重复续算、来源感知的进程管理、自适应评估和偶发事件解释，但固定规则无法安全处理所有工作流条件。作者希望避免将每个操作都置于LLM推理循环中，而是在需要判断力的关键节点选择性使用LLM。

Method: 采用"持久规则代理+选择性LLM推理"的双层架构：规则代理负责常规模拟执行、分析、续算、归档和状态推进，遵循既定策略并维护显式状态记录；LLM推理仅在战役构建和事件触发审查时介入。在GCMC-MD水蒸气脱附场景中验证，覆盖5个蒙脱石体系和3个递进相对湿度状态。

Result: 在15个体系-湿度状态组合中完成120段分段模拟循环，常规生产阶段无需实时推理代理调用；一个状态达到审查边界，两个保留事件通过盲测推理重放评估，成功识别底层工作流问题并给出后续行动建议。模拟还发现Ca基蒙脱石保留更多层间水且基底间距更大，而最高电荷的Na基体系在干燥条件下保留更多残余水。

Conclusion: 长时程科学工作流无需将所有操作放入LLM推理循环中；选择性推理与确定性执行、结构化证据和验证过的控制交接相结合，即可实现可复现且可审计的代理辅助分子模拟。

Abstract: Long-running molecular simulation campaigns require repeated continuation from saved states, provenance-aware progression, adaptive assessment, and occasional interpretation of workflow conditions that cannot be resolved safely by fixed rules. Here, we present Agent-MD, a framework that places large language model (LLM) reasoning selectively at campaign construction and event-triggered review, while routine simulation, analysis, continuation, archiving, and state progression are handled by a persistent rule-based campaign agent using approved policies and explicit state records. Agent-MD was demonstrated in a grand canonical Monte Carlo-molecular dynamics (GCMC-MD) water-vapor desorption campaign comprising five montmorillonite systems and three sequential relative-humidity states (RH = 0.9-0.3-0.1). Across 15 system-RH states, the workflow completed 120 segmented simulation cycles with state-specific sampling lengths and provenance-aware restart inheritance. Routine production required no live reasoning-agent invocation, while one state reached a review boundary; two preserved incidents were subsequently evaluated through blinded reasoning-agent replay, which identified the underlying workflow problems and recommended appropriate follow-up actions. The simulations also revealed distinct composition-dependent low-RH responses, with Ca-bearing montmorillonite retaining more interlayer water and maintaining a larger basal spacing than the Na- and K-bearing systems, while the highest-charge Na system retained more residual water under dry conditions. These results demonstrate that long-running scientific workflows need not place every operation inside an LLM reasoning loop: selective reasoning can instead be combined with deterministic execution, structured evidence, and validated control handoffs to provide reproducible and auditable agent-assisted molecular simulation.

</details>


### [95] [Contextual Value Alignment via Multilayer Combinatorial Fusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07642)
*Yuanhong Wu,Djallel Bouneffouf,D. Frank Hsu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出MCF-CVA框架，通过多层组合融合算法（EAR过程）在欧几里得评分空间和Kemeny排序空间中组合多个道德智能体的输出，实现大语言模型的上下文价值对齐，在标准指标上优于单智能体基线、多智能体单层结果及现有聚合方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM价值对齐方法（如RLHF、CAI）依赖单智能体框架和统一奖励系统，难以捕捉伦理多元性、适应多样化道德上下文，也无法反映多智能体道德推理的动态性。

Method: 提出多层组合融合框架MCF-CVA：第一层实例化多个道德智能体（各代表不同价值），通过分数组合和排序组合（平均/加权聚合）扩展输出，再缩减回初始智能体数量，该扩展-缩减（EAR）过程迭代多层直至停止条件，在欧几里得评分空间和Kemeny排序空间的双架构上运行，利用智能体间的认知多样性消解冲突和冗余。

Result: 经验评估表明该框架在标准指标上优于单智能体基线、多智能体单层结果及先前聚合方法，验证了其在上下文价值对齐中的稳健性和有效性。

Conclusion: MCF-CVA通过多层组合融合和认知多样性利用，为大语言模型提供了一种稳健有效的上下文价值对齐机制，弥补了单智能体框架在伦理多元性和动态道德推理方面的不足。

Abstract: Aligning large language models (LLMs) with human values remains a major challenge, especially for trustworthy AI. While existing approaches such as RLHF, CAI, and their variants have achieved promising results, they often rely on a single-agent framework and a unified reward system. This limits their ability to capture ethical pluralism, adapt to diverse moral contexts, and reflect the dynamics of multi-agent moral reasoning.
  In this work, we propose a framework that utilizes multilayer combinatorial fusion for contextual value alignment (MCF-CVA). At the first layer of the framework, it instantiates multiple moral agents, each fine-tuned to represent a distinctive value. Their outputs are then expanded combinatorially using both score- and rank-combinations as well as average and weighted aggregations. These combined models are then reduced to the same number of initial moral agents. This expansion and reduction (EAR) process continues for multi-layers until a stopping criterion is reached.
  The MCF-CVA framework leverages cognitive diversity between agents to mitigate conflicts and redundancies across multiple agents, producing responses that better reflect contextual human values. The framework using the EAR algorithm is performed on the dual architecture of Euclidean score space and Kemeny rank space. Empirical evaluations demonstrated that the proposed framework outperforms single-agent baselines, multi-agent single-layer results, and previous aggregation approaches on standard metrics, showing that the MCF-CVA framework provides a robust and effective mechanism for advancing contextual value alignment in LLMs.

</details>


### [96] [Mendel Gödel Machine: Recursive Self-Improving Coding Agents via Comparative Evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07645)
*Changzhi Liu,Yilun Liu,Sikuan Yan,Volker Tresp,Yunpu Ma*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Mendel Gödel Machine (MGM)，一种基于孟德尔遗传原理的自改进编码智能体框架，通过引入反应规范变异（基于多任务轨迹的修改）和跨谱系杂交（基于他智能体同任务轨迹的修改）两种新的自修改机制，超越传统的基于单条失败轨迹的克隆变异方法，在理论、模拟和实际编码基准上均取得更优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的自改进编码智能体每次只从单条失败轨迹中学习，忽视了智能体历史尝试存档中蕴含的丰富对比信息——跨任务和跨智能体的轨迹可以为本轮修改提供更有价值的信号。

Method: 借鉴孟德尔遗传学中受控遗传的原理，MGM在传统单轨迹克隆变异基础上新增两种自修改策略：(1) 反应规范变异——基于智能体在多个任务上的轨迹同时进行编辑；(2) 跨谱系杂交——利用另一谱系参考智能体在相同任务上的轨迹进行编辑。论文在加性适应度景观模型下进行了理论证明和代理仿真验证。

Result: 理论分析证明新策略在加性适应度景观下比单轨迹基线收敛更快且收敛性更好；在SWE-bench和Polyglot基准上的实验确认MGM在性能、效率和泛化性方面均有一致的提升。

Conclusion: 利用多轨迹对比信息（跨任务与跨智能体）的自修改策略能够显著提升自改进编码智能体的表现，MGM为这一方向提供了理论与实证支持。

Abstract: Self-improving coding agents that iteratively rewrite their own source code have demonstrated impressive performance on coding tasks. However, existing solutions generally derive self-modification from a single failure trajectory at a time, overlooking rich comparative signals available in the agent's expanding archive of past attempts. According to Mendelian principles of controlled inheritance, we introduce Mendel Gödel Machine (MGM). In addition to the general single-trajectory clonal mutation, MGM includes two new types of self-modification that better utilizes evidences accumulated: the reaction-norm mutation edits an agent based on its trajectories on multiple tasks simultaneously, and the cross-lineage hybridization edits an agent using the trajectory of a reference agent from another lineage on the same task. Under an additive fitness landscape model, we prove theoretically and demonstrate via controlled surrogate simulation that the new strategies facilitate a faster and better convergence over single-trajectory baselines. Experiments on SWE-bench and Polyglot confirm MGM's consistent improvement in performance, efficiency, and generalizability.

</details>


### [97] [An Agentic AI Framework Overcomes Fundamental Limitations of Large Language Models for Glaucoma Detection from Fundus Photography](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07651)
*Jalil Jalili,Hossein Taghizad,Anuwat Jiravarnsirikul,Christopher Bowd,Akram Belghith,Raheleh Kafieh,Christopher A. Girkin,Sally L. Baxter,Robert N. Weinreb,Linda M. Zangwill,Mark Christopher*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究开发并验证了一个将大语言模型（LLM）与专用深度学习工具整合的代理式AI框架，用于眼底照相的青光眼检测。该工作流包含三个步骤：LLM初始评估、函数调用以激活图像质量评估/分类/分割工具、以及结合工具输出的LLM反思。在两种LLM（Gemini 2.5 Flash, GPT-5.4 mini）和两个公开数据集上验证，代理式工作流将分类准确率提升了16-47个百分点，杯盘比误差下降15-50%，运行一致性从近乎随机的kappa(-0.01)提升到近乎完美的kappa(0.96)。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在医学图像解读中虽有前景，但存在幻觉、准确性有限和运行间不一致等关键缺陷。这些局限严重制约了LLM在青光眼检测等临床任务中的可靠应用，需要一种能够弥补这些缺陷的框架。

Method: 构建了一个三层代理式AI工作流：(1) LLM进行初始评估；(2) 通过函数调用调用专门的深度学习工具——图像质量模型（QAModel, FundaQ-8）、青光眼分类模型（SwinV2-Tiny）和视盘/视杯分割模型（SegFormer-B0）；(3) LLM反思阶段将初始印象与工具输出整合。在ORIGA和RIM-ONE-v3两个公开数据集上，以未裁剪和裁剪视野两种条件，由盲法青光眼专家进行独立标注评估。

Result: 代理式工作流在所有条件下将分类准确率提升16-47个百分点，最佳配置在RIM-ONE-v3上达到与专家持平的88%准确率。纯LLM方法暴露两类失败：GPT-5.4 mini呈现阳性偏倚（敏感性95-100%，特异性0-5%），Gemini 2.5 Flash呈随机运行间波动。杯盘比MAE从0.156-0.228降至0.104-0.132，与专家的相关性从弱（r=0.12-0.39）升至中强（r=0.59-0.84）。运行一致性kappa从低至-0.01升至高达0.96。两种LLM均有增益，表明跨骨干网络的泛化性。

Conclusion: 将LLM与专用工具整合的代理式AI框架有效解决了LLM单独使用时的过度诊断与运行间变异性问题。结果在两种LLM骨干上均成立，预示医学AI可能从单体模型向编排式多智能体系统转变。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) show promise in medical image interpretation but suffer from hallucination, limited accuracy, and run-to-run inconsistency. We developed and validated an agentic AI framework integrating LLMs with specialized deep learning tools for glaucoma detection from fundus photography. The workflow had three steps: (1) LLM initial assessment; (2) function calling to invoke specialized tools for image quality (QAModel, FundaQ-8), glaucoma classification (SwinV2-Tiny), and optic disc/cup segmentation (SegFormer-B0); and (3) LLM reflection integrating the initial impression with tool outputs. Two LLMs (Gemini 2.5 Flash, GPT-5.4 mini) were evaluated on two public datasets (ORIGA, n=100; RIM-ONE-v3, n=100) under uncropped and cropped fields of view; all images were independently graded by a masked fellowship-trained glaucoma specialist. The agentic workflow improved classification accuracy by 16 to 47 percentage points across all conditions, reaching within 6 points of the specialist; on RIM-ONE-v3 the best configurations matched the specialist accuracy of 88%. LLM-alone approaches failed in two ways: GPT-5.4 mini showed positive bias (sensitivity 95-100%, specificity 0-5%), while Gemini 2.5 Flash varied stochastically between runs; the agentic workflow corrected both. Cup-to-disc ratio error fell 15-50% (MAE 0.156-0.228 to 0.104-0.132), and correlation with specialist grading rose from weak (r=0.12-0.39) to moderate-strong (r=0.59-0.84). Run-to-run consistency rose from near-random (kappa as low as -0.01) to near-perfect (kappa up to 0.96). Integrating LLMs with specialized tools addressed key limitations of LLM-alone approaches, including over-diagnosis and run-to-run variability. Gains held for both LLMs, suggesting generalizability across backbones, and may signal a shift from monolithic models toward orchestrated multi-agent systems in medical AI.

</details>


### [98] [IntelliAudit: Using Large Language Models to Evaluate Audit Controls](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07688)
*Allison Wilson,Sina Moradi Sabet,Diar Shakimov,Panteha Shahrivar,Mohammad Reza Bagheri,Dean Konenkamp,Mohammad A. Tayebi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: IntelliAudit是一个基于检索的多智能体系统，用于IT审计证据评估。该系统能够检索相关工件、生成基于证据的评估、质疑不利发现、裁决分歧，并产生面向审计师的建议（含引用证据、理由、缺失证据分析和整改指导）。在ISO/IEC 27001上的评估表明其可辅助控制解读、证据推理和审计准备工作，但强调人工监督对校准充分性判断和纠正过度宽松建议的重要性，应作为决策支持工具而非自主认证系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: IT审计要求审计师判断异构组织证据是否满足语义安全和合规控制。这一判断难以自动化，因为相关证据分散在策略、记录、电子表格和运维工件中，且审计结论取决于证据充分性而非关键词匹配。

Method: IntelliAudit采用检索增强的多智能体架构：给定控制项和证据语料库，系统依次执行证据检索、基于证据的评估生成、对不利发现提出质疑、分歧裁决，最终输出面向审计师的建议（含引用证据、理由、缺失证据分析和整改指导）。该系统在ISO/IEC 27001框架上实例化，并在多个模拟组织中通过专家审计师评审和审计就绪度用户反馈进行评估。

Result: 评估表明IntelliAudit能够支持控制解读、证据推理和审计准备工作流，同时也揭示了人工监督在校准充分性判断和纠正过度宽松建议方面的重要性。

Conclusion: 检索增强的多智能体系统可以辅助审计证据审查，但应保持在决策支持工具的定位，而非作为自主认证系统使用。

Abstract: IT audits require auditors to judge whether heterogeneous organizational evidence satisfies semantic security and compliance controls. This judgment is difficult to automate because relevant evidence is distributed across policies, records, spreadsheets, and operational artifacts, and because audit conclusions depend on evidentiary sufficiency rather than keyword matching. We present IntelliAudit, a retrieval-grounded multi-agent system for IT audit evidence evaluation. Given a control and an evidence corpus, IntelliAudit retrieves relevant artifacts, generates an evidence-grounded assessment, challenges adverse findings, adjudicates disagreements, and produces an auditor-facing recommendation with cited evidence, rationale, missing-evidence analysis, and remediation guidance. We instantiate IntelliAudit on ISO/IEC 27001 and evaluate it across multiple simulated organizations using expert auditor review and audit-readiness user feedback. The evaluation shows that IntelliAudit can support control interpretation, evidence-grounded reasoning, and audit-preparation workflows, while also revealing the importance of human oversight for calibrating sufficiency judgments and correcting overly permissive recommendations. These results suggest that retrieval-grounded multi-agent systems can assist audit evidence review, but should remain decision-support tools rather than autonomous certification systems.

</details>


### [99] [Towards Researcher Agents for Knowledge-Graph Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07700)
*Tommaso Soru,Abdulsobur Oyewale*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一个智能体文本转SPARQL系统，其研究者智能体能在验证集上每轮推理后自行提议并测试对自身提示、规则和工具编排代码的修改，在DBpedia上迭代九次自改进版本，最终在2025验证集上达到0.22整体准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将自然语言问题翻译为可在大型知识图谱上执行的SPARQL查询需要解决词汇歧义、将表面词锚定到目标本体，并生成语法有效且语义忠实的图模式。现有静态工具调用智能体无法适应这些挑战，需要更灵活的自改进机制。

Method: 提出一种超越静态工具调用智能体的智能体式文本转SPARQL系统，核心是"研究者智能体"（researcher agent），它在验证集上每轮推理后自行提议并测试对自身提示、规则和工具编排代码的修改，形成自我改进循环。在DBpedia上实例化该循环，利用低成本推理模型驱动九次连续版本进化，并用两个更强的骨干模型部署最优配置。

Result: 研究发现三点：(i) 自改进快速收敛，最终在2025 DBpedia验证集上达到0.22整体准确率；(ii) 瓶颈始终是基本图模式的谓词选择，而非SPARQL语法或修饰符；(iii) 部分基准条目因DBpedia属性歧义而惩罚了正确查询，建议未来Text-to-SPARQL基准应结合机器翻译和信息检索指标进行评分。

Conclusion: 该研究表明智能体自我改进是提升文本转SPARQL性能的有效策略，但当前基准的评估方式存在不足，需要更公平的评分指标体系来准确反映系统能力。

Abstract: Translating a natural-language question into a SPARQL query that can be executed against a large knowledge graph requires resolving lexical ambiguity, grounding surface terms in the target ontology, and producing graph patterns that are both syntactically valid and semantically faithful. We present an agentic text-to-SPARQL system that goes one step beyond static tool-using agents: a researcher agent that, after each round of inference on a validation set, proposes and tests changes to its own prompts, rules, and tool-orchestration code. We instantiate the loop on DBpedia, evolve nine successive versions of the agent driven by a low-cost reasoning model, and deploy the best-performing configuration with two stronger backbone models. The study yields three observations: (i) self-improvement converges quickly and then achieves 0.22 overall accuracy on the 2025 DBpedia validation set; (ii) the bottleneck is consistently in basic-graph-pattern predicate selection, not in SPARQL syntax or modifiers; and (iii) several benchmark items appear to penalise correct queries due to property ambiguity in DBpedia, suggesting that future Text-to-SPARQL benchmarks should be scored using a combination of machine translation and information retrieval metrics.

</details>


<div id='cs.RO'></div>

# cs.RO [[Back]](#toc)

### [100] [Generalizing deep reinforcement learning across cable-driven parallel robot configurations with actuator-level policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07546)
*Abir Bouaouda,Mohamed Boutayeb,François Charpillet,Dominique Martinez,Rémi Pannequin*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于深度强化学习（DRL）的缆索驱动并联机器人（CDPR）控制方法，核心创新在于采用执行器级策略（actuator-level policy），即每个电机独立控制其目标缆绳长度，而非传统的端到端控制整个机器人到达指定末端位置。该方法与机器人具体构型无关，单一共享策略可适用于任意CDPR配置和任意电机数量，且依赖于逆运动学而避免了更难的正运动学问题。策略在仿真中训练并成功迁移到实际CDPR系统，实验表明该方法在鲁棒性和精度上均优于传统强化学习方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CDPR具有多样化的构型和复杂的控制挑战，虽可通过DRL学习其非线性动力学，但现有DRL方法训练时间漫长，且学到的策略难以泛化到不同机器人构型或不同数量的执行器。因此，需要一种与机器人具体配置无关、可跨构型泛化的DRL控制方法。

Method: 提出执行器级策略（ALP）的DRL控制方法：训练每个电机独立控制达到其目标缆绳长度，取代传统的端到端位置控制策略。利用逆运动学计算目标缆绳长度，避免了正运动学的求解难题。策略在仿真环境中训练，通过域随机化等手段实现从仿真到真实CDPR的迁移。

Result: 实验结果表明，ALP在鲁棒性和精度方面均优于传统强化学习方法。进一步，作者使用在仿真4电机平面2D CDPR上训练的策略成功控制了真实8电机3D运动的CDPR，证明该方法可适用于任意CDPR构型，与执行器数量和布置无关。

Conclusion: 执行器级策略是首个应用于CDPR控制的DRL方法，打破了策略对机器人构型的限制，实现了跨配置、跨执行器数量的零样本迁移，为CDPR的通用化和规模化控制提供了有效途径。

Abstract: Cable-driven parallel robots (CDPRs) present diverse configurations and complex control challenges, which can be addressed by deep reinforcement learning (DRL) by learning their nonlinear dynamics. However, DRL methods often require extensive training time, and the resulting policies do not generalize well to different robot configurations or varying numbers of actuators. In this article, we introduce a novel DRL approach for controlling CDPRs that does not depend on the specific robot configuration. Our method trains an actuator-level policy that controls each motor to achieve its target cable length, in contrast to conventional DRL approaches that learn to control the entire robot to reach a desired end-effector position. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first work to apply DRL to control CDPRs using an actuator-level policy. This approach offers two main advantages: (i) a single shared policy can be applied to any CDPR configuration, regardless of actuator count, and (ii) reliance on inverse kinematics, avoiding the more challenging forward kinematics problem. Training is performed in simulation, and the learned policy is successfully transferred to a real CDPR. Experimental results show that the actuator-level policy (ALP) surpasses traditional reinforcement learning methods in both robustness and precision. We further control a real 8-motor CDPR with 3D motion using a policy trained on a simulated 4-motor planar CDPR operating in 2D. This illustrates that the proposed method is applicable to any CDPR configuration, independent of actuator number or placement.

</details>


### [101] [SC$^{2}$-WM: A Self-Correcting World Model with Closed-Loop Feedback for Vision-and-Language Navigation in Continuous Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07548)
*Xuan Yao,Yuze Zhu,Junyu Gao,Zongmeng Wang,Changsheng Xu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出SC²-WM，一种自校正世界模型框架，用于连续环境中的视觉语言导航（VLN-CE）。该方法通过世界模型前瞻生成内部反馈，在执行动作前进行状态级计划修正，并引入条件世界感知自适应机制，在反馈指示模型能力不足时选择性地在测试时更新世界模型，实现模型级校正，从而提升导航鲁棒性和泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLN-CE方法大多依赖开环执行，缺乏在推理过程中检测和纠正内部状态漂移的机制，导致在部分可观测环境下难以做出精细的导航决策并容易累积误差。

Method: SC²-WM框架包含两个核心组件：(1) 基于世界模型前瞻的内部反馈机制，在执行动作前进行状态级计划细化，实现闭环决策；(2) 条件世界感知自适应机制，当反馈表明模型容量不足时，在测试时选择性地更新世界模型，实现模型级校正。

Result: 在标准VLN-CE基准上的实验表明，所提方法显著提升了导航鲁棒性和泛化能力。

Conclusion: 通过引入内部反馈驱动的闭环世界模型框架，SC²-WM有效解决了VLN-CE中状态漂移问题，为连续环境下的鲁棒导航提供了新的解决思路。

Abstract: Vision-and-Language Navigation in Continuous Environments (VLN-CE) requires agents to make fine-grained navigation decisions under partial observability. However, most existing methods rely on open-loop execution, lacking mechanisms to detect and correct internal state drift during inference. We propose SC$^{2}$-WM, a self-correcting world model framework that introduces internal feedback for closed-loop decision making in VLN-CE. Our method derives feedback from world-model foresight to perform state-level plan refinement before action execution. To handle challenging scenarios, we further introduce conditional world-aware adaptation, which enables model-level correction by selectively updating the world model at test time when feedback indicates model capacity insufficiency. Experiments on standard VLN-CE benchmarks demonstrate improved navigation robustness and generalization. Our code is available at https://github.com/sunrise-ikun/SC2_WM.

</details>


### [102] [Self Supervised Learning from Automatically Generated Demonstrations for Visual Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07553)
*Andres Rivas,Anselmo R. Cukla,Rodrigo S. Guerra,Bruna V. Guterres,Ricardo B. Grando*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种自监督的视觉操作框架，机器人围绕目标位姿自动生成演示数据，直接从腕部单目RGB图像学习相对位姿修正，无需相机-机器人外部标定。采用ROS 2和Isaac Sim采集标注图像-位姿对，分离平面细化与三维粗接近数据集训练卷积网络。粗到精控制器先在仿真中验证（平面离散度从9.69mm降至5.38mm），再在UR5e真实机器人上对三个物理物体进行端到端抓取，成功率最高达66.6%和63.6%，并展现旋转条件下的部分鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统机器人操作依赖特定物体编程、人工标注或标定感知管线，部署效率低。虽然示教学习提供更直接的替代方案，但收集演示数据仍需人工遥操作或动觉教学。本文旨在消除这些人工依赖，实现自动化演示生成与免标定的视觉操作。

Method: 提出自监督视觉操作管道：机器人围绕目标位姿自动生成演示数据，利用ROS 2和Isaac Sim采集标注图像-位姿对以避免显式相机-机器人外参标定。分别生成平面细化（planar refinement）和三维粗接近（coarse 3D approach）两个数据集，训练卷积网络从单帧RGB图像回归相对平移与旋转。执行阶段采用粗到精控制器：先用含高度变化的模型完成接近，再用平面数据细化最终对齐。

Result: 仿真中细化阶段将平面位姿离散度从9.69mm降至5.38mm。真实UR5e实验中，对三个物理物体的端到端抓取成功率分别为66.6%和63.6%（两个物体无旋转条件），旋转条件下仍保持部分鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 自动生成的演示数据能够以较低的设置开销支持实用的视觉操作任务，验证了免标定自监督方法的可行性。同时暴露了深度预测精度不足和物体依赖泛化能力受限等待解决挑战。

Abstract: Robotic manipulation often requires object specific programming, manual data annotation, or calibrated perception pipelines, which limits rapid deployment in practical settings. Learning from demonstration offers a more direct alternative, but collecting demonstrations can still demand human teleoperation or kinesthetic teaching. This paper presents a self supervised visual manipulation method in which a robot automatically generates demonstrations around a target pose and learns relative pose corrections directly from wrist mounted RGB images. The proposed pipeline uses ROS~2 and Isaac Sim to collect labeled image-pose pairs without requiring explicit camera to robot extrinsic calibration. Separate datasets are generated for planar refinement and coarse three dimensional approach, and a convolutional network is trained to regress relative translation and rotation from single frame RGB observations. During execution, a coarse to fine controller first approaches the object using models trained with height variation and then refines the final alignment using planar data. The method is evaluated both in simulation and on a real UR5e collaborative robot equipped with a gripper and a monocular camera. In simulation, the refinement stage reduces the final planar dispersion from 9.69 mm to 5.38 mm. In real world experiments, the system performs end to end grasp attempts on three physical objects and reaches success rates of 66.6% and 63.6% for two objects without object rotation, while still maintaining partial robustness under rotated conditions. These results show that automatically generated demonstrations can support practical visual manipulation with limited setup effort, while also exposing remaining challenges in depth prediction and object dependent generalization.

</details>


### [103] [You Don't Need To Stay in The Loop: An Agentic Robotics Loop for Robot-Policy Improvement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07555)
*Hang Yu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出AgenticRobotics，一个后端无关的机器人策略改进控制平面，借鉴编码智能体的多代理架构，以LLM控制器驱动可丢弃工作线程通过持久化的训练-评估-改进事务来完成机器人策略改进，并通过证据门控、提交键崩溃恢复等机制实现"可离开操作员"的自主运行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 编码智能体（如Claude Code、Codex）已成功闭环软件循环，但机器人策略改进领域缺乏类似架构。核心差异在于：机器人工具（训练策略、训练流水线、数据采集）会频繁失败，因此必须对每次工具调用进行质量度量、记录，并在底层工件变更时使旧工具失效。这需要一个专门的系统来保证自主运行的可靠性和可验证性。

Method: 提出AgenticRobotics控制平面架构，包含：(1) 不可变目标（objective）；(2) 控制器持有的测量机制；(3) 提交键崩溃恢复；(4) 证据分级技能库；(5) 带标准化记录调用面的工具注册表。LLM控制器通过持久的训练-评估-改进事务驱动可丢弃的工作线程，采用后端无关设计。

Result: 实验结果表明：证据门控将虚假晋升率从0.005-0.021（未加固）降至0.001（加固后），实现任意时点终止下的任意时刻有效决策，在killed注入下零效果丢失或重复，签名验证器能捕获全部六类工件篡改情形。

Conclusion: 该工作的标题是操作性声明而非选择性声明：操作员可以离开是因为晋升受证据门控、状态可恢复、能力质量从记录推导而来——而非因为循环选择了比人类更好的检查点（在作者测量的单系谱上，它并没有）。但门控机制在错误控制、崩溃恢复和防篡改方面带来了可量化的保障。

Abstract: Coding agents such as Claude Code and Codex close the software loop: a main agent manages the loop, subagents analyze and execute, tools do the work. We port this architecture to robot-policy improvement, where one difference dominates the design: robotic tools---trained policies, training pipelines, data collection---fail routinely, so a tool's quality must be measured, recorded at every call, and expired when the artifact behind it changes. AgenticRobotics is a backend-independent control plane in which an LLM controller drives disposable workers through durable train--evaluate--improve transactions: an immutable objective, controller-owned measurement, commit-keyed crash recovery, an evidence-graded skill library, and a tool registry with a standardized, recorded call surface. The title is an operational claim, not a selection claim: the operator can leave because promotion is evidence-gated, state is recoverable, and capability quality is derived from records---not because the loop picks better checkpoints than a human; on the one lineage we measured, it does not. The gates measurably buy false-promotion control (0.001 per run hardened versus 0.005--0.021 shipped), anytime-valid decisions under optional stopping, zero lost or duplicate effects under kill injection, and six of six artifact-tampering classes caught by a signed verifier.

</details>


### [104] [High Fidelity Capture, Reconstruction, and Transfer of Human Demonstrations for Robot-Assisted Bathing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09127)
*Arjun S. Lakshmipathy,Jonathan P. King,Ethan Zuo,Rohit Satishkumar,Hongyi Chen,Jeffrey Ichnowski,Dan Ding,Zackory Erickson,Nancy S. Pollard*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一个以接触区域为核心处理原语的框架，用于采集、理解并迁移高动态、接触密集的人体洗澡演示数据，并利用该数据集驱动一个机械臂搭载的软体手在人体模型上完成洗澡任务，实现了开放和闭环控制策略下的有效迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管机器人在临床洗澡等高价值任务中需求巨大，但现有系统缺乏与人类进行复杂、持续物理交互所需的安全性和可靠性。其核心障碍在于：即使借助现代运动与触觉传感设备，采集、理解并有效迁移高动态、接触密集的人体洗澡演示数据仍然非常困难。

Method: 提出一个以接触区域作为关键处理原语的简洁高效框架，用于高保真地采集和处理人类洗澡演示数据。基于该框架构建由训练有素的临床医生在真人受试者上执行的洗澡演示数据集，包含同步的高质量运动、形状、接触和力信息。随后利用该数据集，通过开放和闭环控制策略，设计并控制一个机械臂搭载的灵巧软体手在人体模型上执行洗澡任务。

Result: 该数据集是首个在持续、接触密集的人-人交互过程中提供高质量同步运动、形状、接触和力信息的数据集。迁移策略在机器人技术栈的多个层级上展示了这些数据的有效利用，能够在人体模型上成功执行洗澡任务。

Conclusion: 该框架和数据集为物理人机交互（pHRI）研究提供了重要的基础资源，所有相关材料将公开发布，以促进该领域的进一步研究和发展。

Abstract: Despite the demand for robots in high-value clinical tasks like bathing, contemporary systems still lack the safety and reliability required for complex, sustained physical interaction with humans. A key challenge hindering the development of such systems is that collecting, understanding, and effectively transferring highly dynamic, contact-rich human bathing demonstrations is difficult, even with modern motion and tactile sensing equipment. We present a straightforward, but effective framework for doing so with high fidelity by utilizing contact regions as a key processing primitive. We use our framework to build a dataset of bathing demonstrations performed by trained clinicians on human subjects. We then use this dataset to design and control an arm-mounted dexterous soft hand to perform bathing tasks on a mannequin using open- and closed-loop strategies. Our dataset is the first to provide high quality synchronized motion, shape, contact, and force during sustained, contact-rich human-human interaction, and our transfer strategies demonstrate effective use of these data across multiple levels of the robotics stack. All relevant materials will be publicly released to enable further advancements in physical human-robot interaction (pHRI) research.

</details>


### [105] [AeroDPO: Unleashing Lightweight UAV Navigation with High-Fidelity Perception and Automated Preference Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07557)
*Peng Xu,Chengcheng Wang,Shaohua Wan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出AeroDPO，一种零成本的自动化直接偏好优化管线，通过物理模拟状态回滚和特权干预合成碰撞避免偏好数据，使仅2B参数的轻量级无人机视觉-语言导航模型在成功率上与7B大模型持平，同时在未测绘场景中将成功率提升至49.16%并大幅抑制碰撞率，建立新的SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的无人机视觉-语言导航（UAV-VLN）端到端方法通常依赖数十亿参数的大语言模型，推理延迟高，难以满足真实边缘部署需求。同时，纯行为克隆（BC）范式缺乏显式负反馈，导致智能体在分布外（OOD）场景中无法内化空间约束，碰撞率过高。为此，作者挑战"参数越大越好"的假设，并希望在不依赖不可扩展的人工标注的前提下解决BC的鲁棒性缺陷。

Method: AeroDPO包含四个核心步骤：(1) 基于确定性物理仿真状态回滚，在检测到碰撞后自动将环境回滚到碰撞前的状态；(2) 从回滚状态提取因果推理错误的动作作为被拒样本（rejected actions）；(3) 应用解耦的特权干预合成规避碰撞的偏好动作（preferred maneuvers）；(4) 利用离线视觉语言检查器过滤视觉歧义样本。整个过程完全自动化、零人工标注成本，形成一个可持续迭代的数据飞轮。

Result: 跨尺度评估表明感知质量比语言推理能力更关键：2B参数的轻量模型配以高保真视觉输入即可完全匹配7B基线的整体成功率。通过AeroDPO数据飞轮训练后，2B模型在未测绘场景中成功率提升至49.16%，同时大幅抑制碰撞率，达到新的SOTA。

Conclusion: 本文证明了UAV-VLN领域"感知优先、语言从简"的设计理念：轻量模型配合高质量感知与自动化数据增强，可以在不牺牲性能的前提下实现边缘部署友好，并有效克服行为克隆的鲁棒性缺陷，为自主空中智能体的实用化部署提供了新路径。

Abstract: Vision-Language Navigation for Unmanned Aerial Vehicles (UAV-VLN) requires rapid and reactive control in complex 3D environments. Recent minimalist end-to-end paradigms show great promise but typically rely on massive language models containing billions of parameters, incurring prohibitive latency for real-world edge deployment. In this paper, we challenge this parameter-heavy reliance. Comprehensive cross-scale evaluations reveal the critical insight that perception quality fundamentally outweighs language reasoning capacity. We demonstrate that a lightweight 2B model equipped with high-fidelity visual inputs completely matches the overall success rates of massive 7B baselines. However, this minimalist policy exposes a fundamental robustness flaw inherent to pure Behavior Cloning (BC). Lacking explicit negative feedback, the agent fails to internalize robust spatial constraints and exhibits alarming collision rates in out-of-distribution (OOD) scenarios. To overcome this vulnerability without relying on unscalable human annotations, we propose AeroDPO, a zero-cost automated Direct Preference Optimization pipeline driven by deterministic physical simulation state rollback. Upon detecting collisions, the system autonomously rewinds the environment to extract causal reasoning errors as rejected actions, applies decoupled privileged interventions to synthesize collision-avoidance preferred maneuvers, and leverages an offline vision language inspector to filter visual ambiguities. By equipping our 2B model with this automated data flywheel, AeroDPO boosts success rates to 49.16% on unmapped scenarios while drastically suppressing collision rates, establishing a new SOTA for autonomous aerial agents.

</details>


### [106] [Learning Physical Interaction: A Survey of Tactile- and Force-aware Robot Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07558)
*Shilin Shan,Chuhao Zhou,Ruize Wang,Xinyan Chen,Xiangyu Chen,Xinyu Zhou,Boyu Ma,Iris Yuxuan Hu,Jingliang Li,Celeste Yuxuan Hu,Geng Li,Guohao Chen,Tianrui Zhu,Zhe Li,Yanjie Ze,Haoran Geng,Zhiyang Dou,Jianxin Bi,Yuejiang Liu,Jianshu Zhou,Jiachen Li,Paul Liang,Tatsuya Harada,Robert Katzschmann,Harold Soh,Na Li,Edward Johns,Danica Kragic,Jan Peters,Wojciech Matusik,Masayoshi Tomizuka,Jitendra Malik,Jianfei Yang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出TF-ART分类体系，从统一视角综述力觉和触觉感知的机器人学习，涵盖多模态感知与多阶段系统设计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 物理交互能力对机器人操作至关重要，尤其在接触敏感的操作任务中需要力觉与触觉感知。然而，现有综述缺乏从统一视角整合多模态感知与多阶段系统设计的力觉/触觉感知机器人学习综述。

Method: 提出TF-ART（Tactile/Force-Aware Robot learning Taxonomy）分类框架，将现有方法映射到统一的分层结构中，从观测模态组织、异构感知输入编码与融合、多阶段动作生成与精化、以及学习策略与机器人末端反应控制之间的连接等多个维度进行系统化分类。

Result: 该综述通过TF-ART框架系统地梳理了近年来力觉/触觉感知机器人学习方法，并进一步考察了物理交互的任务设置和基础设施需求，整合了算法与实践两个视角。

Conclusion: TF-ART填补了力觉/触觉感知机器人学习缺乏统一综述的空白，为多模态感知与多阶段系统设计的交叉领域提供了体系化的分析框架。

Abstract: Physically grounded robot intelligence requires robots to perceive, reason about, and regulate their interactions with the physical world. This capability is particularly critical in contact-sensitive manipulation, where successful task execution depends not only on visual perception and motion generation, but also on force regulation and adaptive control. In this context, recent robot learning methods have made substantial progress by integrating force, tactile, vision, language, and proprioceptive sensing into learned manipulation policies. In parallel, many systems adopt multi-phase architectures that combine high-level policies, action-refinement modules, and low-level controllers to bridge semantic task understanding with reactive physical execution. Despite these advances, existing surveys have not explicitly reviewed force- and tactile-aware robot learning from a unified perspective that jointly captures multimodal sensing and multi-phase system design. This survey addresses this gap by proposing TF-ART, a Tactile/Force-Aware Robot learning Taxonomy for multimodal and multi-phase frameworks, which maps individual methods into a unified hierarchical structure. The framework characterizes how recent works organize observation modalities, encode and fuse heterogeneous sensory inputs, generate and refine actions across multiple phases, and connect learned policies to reactive robot-end control. Building on this methodological view, we further examine the task settings and infrastructure requirements of physical interaction, thereby integrating both algorithmic and practical perspectives on force- and tactile-aware robot learning.

</details>


### [107] [Projection-Retraction MPPI: Exact Constraint-Manifold Control for Manipulators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07573)
*Seulchan Lee,Leesai Park,Minhyeong Kang,Sanghyun Kim*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出 Projection-Retraction MPPI (PR-MPPI)，通过在采样动力学内部投影速度以硬性满足等式和不等式约束，并将返回指令回缩到约束流形上，从而解决 MPPI 控制中约束只能作为软惩罚近似满足的问题。在 14-DoF 双臂系统仿真和 Unitree H1-2 实体人形机器人上验证了约束的硬满足性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MPPI 控制广泛应用于操作任务，因其无梯度且能并行处理非凸代价。但操作任务常包含全程约束（如双机械臂形成的闭合运动链、关节限位和障碍物间隙），MPPI 只能将这些约束作为代价中的软惩罚处理，近似满足且在高任务代价下容易失效。

Method: PR-MPPI 在采样动力学内部强制约束。在每个 rollout 步中，将采样速度投影以满足两类约束：等式约束将速度限制到子空间，每个不等式约束限制为子空间内的半空间，从而不等式处理不会破坏等式。由于该投影仅满足一阶约束，有限步会留下偏离等式的小漂移，因此再将返回指令回缩到约束流形上，达到数值容差且与任务加权无关。

Result: 在 14-DoF 双臂系统上进行验证：仿真中，返回指令在关节限位压力测试和随机避障中使闭合链等式满足到数值容差；真实硬件上，Unitree H1-2 人形机器人的双臂对移动障碍物做出反应式避让。

Conclusion: PR-MPPI 能硬性地满足全程等式与不等式约束而不受任务代价强度影响，在仿真和真实硬件上均验证了其约束保持的可靠性与实用性。

Abstract: Model Predictive Path Integral (MPPI) control is widely used in manipulation for its gradient-free, parallel handling of non-convex costs. Manipulation tasks, however, often impose constraints that hold throughout the motion: a closed kinematic chain that two grasping arms keep exactly, or joint limits and obstacle clearances that are never crossed. MPPI handles such constraints only through the cost, as soft penalties that hold approximately and fail under a strong task cost. To address this, we propose Projection-Retraction MPPI (PR-MPPI), which enforces the constraints inside the sampled dynamics. At every rollout step, the sampled velocity is projected to satisfy both constraint types: the equality restricts it to a subspace, and each inequality to a half-space within that subspace, so inequality handling never breaks the equality. This projection, however, satisfies the constraints only to first order, and a finite step leaves a small drift off the equality. Therefore, we retract the returned command back onto the constraint to numerical tolerance and independent of task weighting. We validate PR-MPPI on 14-DoF dual-arm systems. In simulation, the returned commands satisfy the closed-chain equality to numerical tolerance through a joint-limit stress test and randomized obstacle avoidance. On real hardware, the arms of a Unitree H1-2 humanoid reactively avoid a moving obstacle. Code and experiment videos are available at https://rcilab.github.io/prmppi.

</details>


### [108] [Reconfigurable Structural Robotic Assembly: Interlocking 3D Aggregations with Self-Aligning Compound Nested Lattice Modules](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07576)
*Alexander Htet Kyaw,Miana Smith,Paul Richard,Neil Gershenfeld*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种自对准的复合嵌套晶格模块（由共轭的立方八面体-八面体单元组成），将几何智能编码于材料系统中，同时满足机器人抓取、自对准、可逆连接、结构性能和三维聚合需求。通过机械臂和移动组装器成功组装了家具和建筑尺度的结构（座椅、跨距结构、表面和垂直框架），压缩测试显示刚度4,556 N/mm，最大载荷3,445 N，压缩模量17.5 MPa，模块可拆卸重用，支持可重构和循环建筑。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器人建造系统通常将材料系统和机器人视为分离的设计问题，智能主要分布在硬件、传感、运动规划和控制中。本文旨在探索如何将几何智能编码进建筑材料系统中，使材料本身同时满足机器人抓取、自对准、可逆连接、结构性能和三维聚合等多重需求，从而简化机器人建造流程并实现可重构的循环建造。

Method: 提出一种自对准的复合嵌套晶格模块，由共轭的立方八面体（cuboctahedral）和八面体（octahedral）单元组成。立方八面体特征提供机器人抓取和对准的明确表面，八面体特征集成螺旋可释放的卡扣连接器和对应受体，嵌套排列实现沿x、y、z轴的三维互锁聚合。通过机械臂和移动组装器进行演示验证。

Result: 成功组装了家具和建筑尺度的结构，包括座椅、跨距结构、表面和垂直框架。压缩测试中复合模块的刚度为4,556 N/mm，最大载荷为3,445 N，压缩模量为17.5 MPa。模块可拆卸并在不同构型间重复使用，支持可重构和循环建造。

Conclusion: 本研究证明了将几何智能嵌入材料系统可有效实现机器人与建筑系统的统一设计，使结构模块本身同时承担抓取界面、对准机制、连接功能和结构性能等多重角色，为可重构、循环的机器人建造提供了一条新路径。

Abstract: Robotic construction systems often treat the material system and the robot as separate design problems, locating intelligence primarily in hardware, sensing, motion planning, and control. This project instead investigates how geometric intelligence can be encoded within architected material systems to simultaneously address requirements for robotic grasping, self-alignment, reversible connection, structural performance, and three-dimensional aggregation. We introduce a self-aligning compound nested lattice module composed of conjoined cuboctahedral-octahedral units. The cuboctahedral features of the modules provide defined surfaces for robotic grasping and alignment, while the octahedral features incorporate screw-releasable snap-fit connectors and corresponding receptors. Additionally, we present a nested arrangement that enables interlocking aggregation along the x, y, and z axes. We demonstrate the system through furniture and architectural scale structures assembled using both a robotic arm and mobile assembler. The resulting configurations include seating, spanning structures, surfaces, and vertical frames. Compression testing of the compound module produced a stiffness of 4,556 N/mm, a maximum load of 3,445 N, and a compressive modulus of 17.5 MPa. The modules can also be disassembled and reused across different configurations, supporting reconfigurable and circular construction.

</details>


### [109] [LIRA: Local Cross-Layer Information Routing for Vision-Language-Action Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07596)
*Zhewei Zhang,Puyue Wang,Guanren Qiao,Yijie Weng,Jiawei Hu,Guo Li,Lujia Wang,Junyan Wang,Tao Gu,Hongliang Lu,Guiliang Liu,Hong Jia,Xinhu Zheng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出LIRA，一种局部跨层动作条件化机制，通过深度感知的信息路由方式来优化VLM特征到动作解码器的接口设计，在不改变骨干架构、动作解码器和监督训练流程的前提下提升了机器人操纵任务的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA模型在将VLM中间特征路由到动作解码器时存在两个问题：一是只暴露表征层级中较窄的一部分，限制了跨深度互补任务证据的获取；二是将每个解码器块与单个VLM层刚性匹配，缺乏灵活性。这一接口设计尚未被充分探索。

Method: LIRA通过深度感知的信息路由将VLM到动作的条件化建模为跨层路由。具体而言：在任务token特征和从中间VLM状态衍生的LIRA Query特征上操作，为每个并行融合块分配一个以对应VLM层为中心的深度对齐局部窗口；并行融合块聚合相邻LIRA Query特征，并与任务token特征及本体感觉输入集成后进行动作预测。路由接口不改变骨干架构、动作解码器和监督训练配方。

Result: 在LIBERO、LIBERO-Plus、CALVIN ABC→D和真实世界操纵任务中，LIRA在相同的0.5B参数配置下相较VLA-Adapter基线在主要聚合指标上均有提升。在LIBERO-Plus零样本迁移中，平均成功率从59.1%提升至78.0%，提高了18.9个百分点，显示了在受控分布偏移下的鲁棒性提升。

Conclusion: LIRA证明了深度感知的跨层信息路由能有效改善VLA模型的特征利用效率，特别是在分布偏移场景下表现出显著的优势，为VLA模型的接口设计提供了新的方向。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models transform representations from pretrained vision-language models (VLMs) into robot actions, yet the interface that routes intermediate VLM features into action decoders remains underexplored. Existing designs either expose only a narrow part of the representation hierarchy or rigidly match each decoder block to one VLM layer, restricting access to complementary task evidence across depths. We introduce LIRA, a local cross-layer action-conditioning mechanism that formulates VLM-to-action conditioning as depth-aware information routing. LIRA operates on task-token features and LIRA Query features derived from intermediate VLM states, then assigns each Parallel Fusion Block a depth-aligned local window centered on its corresponding VLM layer. Parallel Fusion Blocks aggregate neighboring LIRA Query features and integrate them with task-token features and proprioceptive inputs before action prediction. This routing interface leaves the backbone architecture, action decoder, and supervised training recipe unchanged. Across LIBERO, LIBERO-Plus, CALVIN ABC$\rightarrow$D, and real-world manipulation, LIRA improves the principal aggregate metrics over the VLA-Adapter baseline under the same 0.5B-parameter configuration. In zero-shot transfer to LIBERO-Plus, LIRA increases average success from 59.1% to 78.0%, an 18.9-point gain indicating improved robustness under controlled distribution shifts.

</details>


### [110] [AdaDexGrasp: Adaptive Dexterous Grasping via 3D Visuo-Tactile Representation Fusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07600)
*Xirui Liang,Jiaqi Liang,Jingkai Xu,Yuran Wang,Ruochong Li,Yuanpei Chen,Masayoshi Tomizuka,Wei Zhan,Ruihai Wu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一个统一的视觉-触觉融合抓取框架，集成抓取生成、可行性预测与自适应优化，通过将触觉信号与手指身份关联的高效表征，实现接触前后的抓取自适应调整，在仿真与真实环境中提升抓取成功率与泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器人抓取方法主要依赖视觉输入，缺少接触后的触觉引导自适应机制，导致鲁棒性和泛化能力受限。人类通过视觉感知与触觉反馈的无缝整合实现稳定自适应抓取，这一能力在机器人系统中尚难以复现。

Method: 提出统一视觉-触觉融合抓取框架，包含：(1) 高效视觉-触觉联合表征，将触觉信号与手指身份关联以紧密融合物体几何与触觉反馈；(2) 规划阶段的接触感知抓取位姿生成；(3) 接触后的触觉引导精细优化，支持系统推理细粒度手指-物体交互并动态调整抓取。

Result: 在仿真和真实环境中的综合实验表明，该方法显著提升了抓取成功率，并增强了在多样化物体上的泛化能力。

Conclusion: 视觉-触觉融合表征能够弥补纯视觉抓取方法的不足，通过接触后的触觉反馈实现自适应精调，是提升机器人抓取鲁棒性与泛化性的有效途径。

Abstract: Humans achieve stable and adaptive grasps by seamlessly integrating visual perception and tactile feedback, a capability that remains challenging to replicate in robotic systems. Existing robotic grasping approaches predominantly rely on visual inputs and lack mechanisms for tactile-guided adaptation after contact, limiting robustness and generalization. To address this challenge, we propose a unified visuo-tactile-fusion grasping framework that integrates grasp generation, feasibility prediction, and adaptive refinement. At its core, our method introduces an efficient visuo-tactile representation that tightly fuses object geometry with tactile feedback by associating tactile signals with finger identities. This unified representation supports contact-aware grasp pose generation during planning and tactile-guided refinement after contact, enabling the system to reason about fine-grained finger-object interactions and adjust grasps dynamically. Comprehensive experiments in both simulation and real-world environments demonstrate that our approach significantly enhances grasp success rates and generalization across diverse objects.

</details>


### [111] [Enhanced Real-Time 6-DOF Extended Reality Catheter Tracking for Evaluating Potential Improvement in Efficiency, Precision, and Depth Perception for Cardiac Interventions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07606)
*Mohsen Annabestani,Sandhya Sriram,Andrew Kuzemczak,S. Chiu Wong,Alexandros Sigaras,Bobak Mosadegh*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于扩展现实（XR）的6自由度（6-DOF）导管追踪与可视化平台，通过结合机器视觉算法（5-DOF）和3D打印机电编码器（滚动角测量）实现完整的导管运动重建。在20名医学生的概念验证研究中，3D沉浸式可视化相较传统2D视图在任务完成速度上快5倍以上、导管行程缩短约4倍，并显著提升了靶向精度、降低性能变异性，同时改善了操作的平滑性和临床可用性评价。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管3D超声技术不断进步，大多数经皮心脏介入手术仍依赖2D可视化，这限制了操作者的深度感知和空间理解能力，从而影响手术效率和精度。研究旨在通过XR技术解决这一临床痛点。

Method: 系统开发分为两部分：一是基于机器视觉的定制算法实现5-DOF导管追踪（位置与姿态）；二是利用3D打印的机电编码器测量导管滚动角，二者结合实现完整的6-DOF运动重建。在患者特异性的3D心脏模型中提供实时可视化。验证采用概念验证研究设计，招募20名无经验医学生，分别使用3D沉浸式视图和传统2D导管室视角完成6个解剖目标的ICE导管导航任务，并对比时间、行程、精度、变异性和运动学指标。

Result: 3D条件下受试者平均用时54.6秒、行程1,939 mm；2D条件下分别需267.5秒和7,854 mm。3D模式快5倍以上、导管行程节省约5倍，同时提升了靶向精度并降低了性能变异性。主观评价中沉浸式可视化在准确性、速度、可用性和临床价值方面均获得更高评分。运动学分析显示3D模式深度轴导航更平滑，2D用户则依赖反复的纠正性动作。

Conclusion: 研究证明基于XR的可视化平台能显著提升介入手术培训的效率、精度和运动控制能力，为临床实践中3D沉浸式导航的推广提供了初步证据基础。

Abstract: Despite advances in 3D ultrasound, most percutaneous cardiac interventions still rely on 2D visualization, limiting depth perception and spatial understanding. To address this challenge, we developed an Extended Reality (XR)-based platform that enables real-time six-degree-of-freedom (6-DOF) catheter tracking and visualization within a patient-specific 3D heart model. The system combines a custom machine-vision algorithm for 5-DOF catheter tracking with a 3D-printed electromechanical encoder that measures catheter roll, providing complete 6-DOF motion reconstruction. In a proof-of-concept study, 20 novice medical students navigated an intracardiac echocardiography (ICE) catheter to six anatomical targets using either immersive 3D visualization or a conventional 2D cathlab-style view. Participants in the 3D condition completed the task in 54.6 seconds and traveled 1,939 mm on average, compared with 267.5 seconds and 7,854 mm in the 2D condition. Therefore, the XR-based 3D system was more than 5x faster and required ~5x less catheter travel. The 3D mode also improved targeting precision and reduced performance variability. Participants consistently rated immersive visualization higher for accuracy, speed, usability, and clinical value. Kinematic analysis showed smoother depth-axis navigation in 3D, whereas 2D users relied on repeated corrective movements. These findings demonstrate that XR-based visualization can substantially improve procedural training efficiency, precision, and motor control.

</details>


### [112] [GWM-VLA: Geometry-Aware Latent World Modeling for Vision-Language-Action Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07619)
*Yanping Zhao,Hang Yu,Yiwei Wang,Chen Ye,Siyu Tian,Di Zhang,Qingjun Wang,Qian Chen,Junqiao Zhao,Chen Ye,Guang Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: GWM-VLA提出了一种几何感知的潜世界建模框架，通过结合多视角几何感知状态编码、全局上下文条件的目标视角预测和共享潜动作表示，增强VLA模型在视觉和环境变化下的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA模型在视觉和环境变化下性能下降，虽然潜世界建模能提升鲁棒性，但现有方法通常独立编码各相机视角并预测整体场景动态，没有显式建模多视角之间的几何关系，导致鲁棒性提升受限。

Method: GWM-VLA包含三个关键组件：(1) VGGT-Ω多视角聚合编码器，在每个时间步联合聚合多视角观测以构建几何感知的多视角状态；(2) 潜世界模型基于聚合后的patch和register tokens预测选定目标视角（实验中为腕部视角）的下一步patch tokens，无需预测完整多视角状态，保留多视角几何信息；(3) 共享潜动作表示同时作为潜世界模型和flow-matching动作头的条件，使潜预测监督和真值机器人动作监督协同塑造相同的潜动作表示。

Result: 在仿真和真实机器人实验环境中均验证了GWM-VLA的有效性和鲁棒性，表明几何感知的潜世界建模能够提升VLA模型在视觉和环境变化下的性能。

Conclusion: 通过显式建模多视角几何关系并采用目标视角预测策略，GWM-VLA能有效提升VLA模型在分布偏移场景下的鲁棒性，且通过共享潜动作表示实现双重监督协同训练。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models achieve strong robotic manipulation performance but often degrade under visual and environmental shifts. Latent world modeling offers a promising approach to improving robustness, yet existing methods commonly encode camera views independently and predict holistic scene dynamics without explicitly modeling their geometric relationships. We propose GWM-VLA, a geometry-aware latent world modeling framework for VLA learning. GWM-VLA combines geometry-aware multi-view state encoding, global context-conditioned target-view prediction, and shared latent-action representations grounded by robot-action supervision. Specifically, VGGT-$Ω$ jointly aggregates multi-view observations at each timestep to construct geometry-aware multi-view states. The latent world model predicts the next-step patch tokens of a selected target view using patch and register tokens obtained after multi-view aggregation, thereby retaining multi-view geometric information without predicting the complete multi-view state. We use the wrist view as the target in our experiments, placing greater emphasis on end-effector motion and local gripper-object interactions. Finally, the shared latent-action representations condition both the latent world model and the flow-matching action head, allowing latent-prediction supervision and ground-truth robot-action supervision to jointly shape the same latent-action representations. Experiments across both simulation and real-world environments demonstrate the effectiveness and robustness of GWM-VLA.

</details>


### [113] [A Reconfigurable Tracked Robot for Enhanced Obstacle Traversal Through Movable Articulation Point and Internal Mass Relocation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07624)
*Yuki Uda,Yasutaka Nakashima,Motoji Yamamoto,Ayato Kanada*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出TRASER，一种带有可重构脊柱的履带机器人，能够同时移动关节点和内部质心，通过带簧机构实现弯曲区域的局部柔顺性与其余部分的高刚度，显著提升了越障能力，实验展示了分别高达车身长度74%、66%和59%的台阶、悬挂平台和壕沟越障性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 履带机器人在非结构化环境中应用广泛，但其越障能力受限于前端可达性与运动稳定性之间的根本性权衡，现有设计难以同时兼顾两者。

Method: 提出TRASER机器人，采用带簧（tape-spring）机制将柔顺性局部化于弯曲区域，同时保持机体其余部分高刚度；能够同时重新定位关节点和内部质量。建立几何模型和静态模型，分析关节点和质心位置对台阶及壕沟越障性能的影响。

Result: 实验实现台阶越障（74%车身长度）、悬挂平台越障（66%车身长度）和壕沟越障（59%车身长度），据作者所知，这是履带移动机器人中已报道的最高越障能力。

Conclusion: TRASER通过关节点和内部质心的双重可重构设计，有效突破了传统履带机器人越障能力的限制，实现了目前最优的越障性能。

Abstract: Tracked robots are widely used in unstructured environments; however, their obstacle traversal capability is fundamentally limited by a tradeoff between front-end reachability and locomotion stability. This study presents TRASER (Tracked Robot with Articulated Spine for Extended Reach), a reconfigurable tracked robot capable of relocating both its articulation point and internal mass. TRASER employs a tape-spring mechanism that localizes compliance to the bending region while maintaining high stiffness in the remaining body, thereby improving both front-end reachability and center-of-mass (CoM) shifting capability. Geometric and static models are developed to analyze the effects of articulation point and CoM position on step and ditch traversal performances. Experiments demonstrate step traversal, suspended-platform traversal, and ditch traversal of 74\%, 66\%, and 59\% of the robot body length, respectively. To the best of our knowledge, these results represent the highest reported obstacle traversal capabilities among tracked mobile robots.

</details>


### [114] [Hölder Signed Distance: A Differentiable, Signed, Parallelizable Metric for Robotics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07707)
*Felipe Bartelt,Ali Umut Kaypak,Anthony Tzes,Farshad Khorrami,Luciano C. A. Pimenta,Vinicius M. Gonçalves*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的凸多面体间的可微有符号距离函数——Hölder有符号距离。它通过引入可微的最小/最大算子（Hölder最小和Hölder最大），替换经典SDF公式中的min-max算子，实现了闭式（closed-form）计算，避免了迭代算法的收敛问题，并天然支持GPU并行化，适用于机器人运动规划与控制中的实时优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 欧几里得有符号距离函数（SDF）并非处处可微，而现有的可微距离替代方案往往在可微性、符号信息或计算效率之间存在权衡。机器人运动规划与控制需要可微的梯度来实现实时优化，因此亟需一种同时具备可微性、符号信息和高计算效率的距离函数。

Method: 本文首先提出可微的Hölder最小和Hölder最大算子，用于替代经典SDF公式中的min-max算子，从而得到凸多面体间的Hölder有符号距离。该距离以闭式形式计算，不依赖迭代算法，因此不存在收敛问题，且易于GPU并行化。

Result: 通过与现有方法的运行时比较，验证了该距离的计算效率和实际优势。此外，通过机器人机械臂实验证明了该方法在控制应用中的适用性。

Conclusion: Hölder有符号距离作为一种闭式的可微有符号距离函数，同时兼顾了可微性、符号信息和计算高效性，有望成为机器人运动规划与控制中实时优化的有力工具。

Abstract: Computing distances between sets is essential in robotic motion planning and control, where differentiable gradients enable real-time optimization. The Euclidean Signed Distance Function (SDF), however, is not differentiable everywhere, and existing alternatives often sacrifice differentiability, sign information, or computational efficiency. In this letter, we introduce a novel differentiable signed distance between convex polyhedra. To this end, we first propose differentiable versions of the minimum and maximum operators, termed the Hölder minimum and Hölder maximum. We then replace the original min-max operators in the classical SDF formulation, yielding the Hölder signed distance. Unlike prior differentiable distance formulations that rely on iterative algorithms, our approach is computed in closed form, eliminating convergence issues while remaining naturally amenable to GPU parallelization. We validate the practical advantages and computational performance of the proposed distance through runtime comparisons with existing approaches. We also present a robotic manipulator experiment, demonstrating its suitability for applications in control.

</details>


### [115] [Complete, Scalable, and Robust Prioritized Planning for Multi-Robot Ordered Storage and Retrieval at Maximum Capacity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07734)
*William Zhang,Tzvika Geft,Jingjin Yu,Kostas Bekris*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文针对自动化仓库中高密度存储与高吞吐检索的权衡问题，提出了一种新的多机器人有序存取问题（OSRP-MC）公式化方法。通过利用无重排存储布局的特性，设计了一种在线、优先级的MAPF（多智能体路径规划）算法，在保证完备性和无死锁的前提下实现可扩展性，实验结果展示了接近线性的makespan改进，并且鲁棒性方案的算法开销可忽略不计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动化仓库面临存储密度与检索吞吐量之间的根本性权衡。PBS（拼图式存储）架构通过消除通道提高了容量，但多机器人在高密度空间中的协调因死锁风险而计算困难。现有通用集中式规划器难以兼顾几何可行性与执行效率，因此需要一种既能保证完备性又能防止死锁的可扩展方法。

Method: 首先将问题形式化为"最大容量下的有序存储与检索问题"（OSRP-MC），针对从单一边界访问的矩形网格。核心思想是利用无重排（relocation-free）存储布局的不变性质，据此设计一种在线的、基于优先级的MAPF算法。该方法不采用通用集中式规划器，而是利用存储排列的具体不变量来保证完备性并避免死锁，从而提升可扩展性。

Result: 实验表明，该方法在makespan随机器人数量增长时实现了接近线性的改进（最多可达m=C，其中C为网格宽度）。同时，为支持鲁棒性所需的算法开销可忽略不计：系统使用鲁棒存储布局处理出发序列的不确定性，与不鲁棒的基线相比在执行速度上没有显著损失。

Conclusion: 该工作弥合了几何可行性与执行效率之间的差距，通过利用无重排布局的特定不变量，实现了在最大容量存储场景下的可扩展多机器人协调，同时无需牺牲执行速度即可获得对出发序列不确定性的鲁棒性。

Abstract: Automated warehouses face a fundamental trade-off between maximizing storage density and achieving high retrieval throughput. While puzzle-based storage (PBS) architectures increase capacity by eliminating aisles, coordinating multiple robots in these high-density spaces is computationally challenging due to the potential for deadlocks. This paper introduces a novel multi-robot formulation for the ``ordered storage and retrieval problem at maximum capacity''. The focus is on rectangular grids accessible from a single boundary, where loads need to be first stored up to full capacity and then efficiently retrieved, given a planned departure sequence. This work bridges the gap between geometric feasibility and execution efficiency by leveraging the properties of relocation-free arrangements. These properties guide an online, prioritized multi-agent path-finding algorithm, which is the main contribution of this work. Unlike general centralized planners, the approach exploits the specific invariants of the storage arrangement to guarantee completeness and prevent deadlocks, enabling scalability. Experiments demonstrate that the method achieves near-linear improvement in makespan with respect to the number of robots, up to $m = C$, where $C$ is the grid width. Crucially, the algorithmic overhead of supporting robustness is negligible; the system handles uncertainty in departure sequences using robust storage arrangements with no significant penalty in execution speed compared to the non-robust baseline.

</details>


### [116] [Enhancing Autonomous Vehicle Navigation with a Clothoid-Based Lateral Controller](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07740)
*Aashish Shaju,Steve Southward,Mehdi Ahmadian*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于回旋曲线（clothoid/Euler spiral）的自适应前瞻距离横向控制策略，用于自动驾驶车辆的路径跟踪与横向稳定性控制，并通过TruckSim和Simulink联合仿真验证了其在多样化驾驶场景下的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统横向控制方法在曲率突变时会导致乘客不适和车辆侧翻风险，且固定前瞻距离难以适应复杂多变的道路几何和车辆动态特性，亟需一种能够实现平滑曲率过渡并自适应调节控制参数的横向控制策略。

Method: 采用回旋曲线（Euler螺线）生成平滑曲率过渡路径；提出基于实时车辆动力学和道路几何的自适应前瞻距离调节机制；在每个时间步通过准反馈控制算法构建最优clothoid并生成转向指令；引入超前滤波器（lead filter）补偿车辆横向动力学延迟，提升控制响应速度与稳定性。

Result: 通过TruckSim与Simulink的联合仿真，在多类驾驶场景下验证了所提出的控制策略在横向控制性能上的显著提升，包括更高的路径跟踪精度和更好的横向稳定性。

Conclusion: 所提出的基于clothoid的自适应前瞻横向控制策略在路径跟踪精度和横向稳定性方面表现优异，未来将扩展到高速场景并针对铰接式车辆进一步优化以减少横向偏移误差。

Abstract: This study introduces an advanced lateral control strategy for autonomous vehicles using a clothoid-based approach integrated with an adaptive lookahead mechanism. The primary focus is on enhancing lateral stability and path-tracking accuracy through the application of Euler spirals for smooth curvature transitions, thereby reducing passenger discomfort and the risk of vehicle rollover. An innovative aspect of our work is the adaptive adjustment of lookahead distance based on real-time vehicle dynamics and road geometry, which ensures optimal path following under varying conditions. A quasi-feedback control algorithm constructs optimal clothoids at each time step, generating the appropriate steering input. A lead filter compensates for the vehicle's lateral dynamics lag, improving control responsiveness and stability. The effectiveness of the proposed controller is validated through a comprehensive co-simulation using TruckSim and Simulink, demonstrating significant improvements in lateral control performance across diverse driving scenarios. Future directions include scaling the controller for higher-speed applications and further optimization to minimize off-track errors, particularly for articulated vehicles.

</details>


### [117] [CoCoNav: Conformal Control for Safe Robot Navigation in Crowds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07751)
*Cheng Guo,Mingzhe Ni,Zheng Liang,Yihu Ling,Yuan Hu,Michele Caprio,Daniele Pucci,Wei Pan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: CoCoNav是一个人群导航框架，结合在线共形校准与运行时认证规划，通过水平特定的共形比例-积分控制器自适应调整轨迹误差边界，并采用"先松弛后验证"的规划器在保证求解器可行性的同时验证轨迹安全性，在仿真和四足机器人实验中取得了碰撞避免、任务成功率与导航效率的良好平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有反应式导航方法易产生振荡行为，而预测性规划器往往将预测视为精确或依赖受限的误差模型。将保守不确定性集作为硬约束会使模型预测控制(MPC)不可行。需要一种既能应对预测误差变化、又能保证求解器可行性且具有安全性保证的人群导航框架。

Method: 提出CoCoNav框架：(1) 在线共形校准——采用水平特定的共形比例-积分(PI)控制器，自适应调整轨迹误差边界，以调节长期经验覆盖率，应对变化的预测误差；(2) "先松弛后验证"(relax-then-verify)规划器——使用软约束MPC生成名义轨迹以保证求解器可行性，然后根据校准后的误差边界，将名义轨迹与应急机动(contingency maneuvers)一起进行独立认证，通过后才执行。

Result: 在仿真和四足机器人(quadruped)实验中，CoCoNav在碰撞避免、任务成功率和导航效率三个指标上相比评估的基线方法取得了更有利的平衡。

Conclusion: CoCoNav通过将共形校准与可验证规划相结合，在保持求解器可行性的同时提供了运行时安全性保障，有效应对人群环境中不断变化的预测误差，为实际机器人人群导航提供了一种兼顾安全与效率的解决方案。

Abstract: Safe and efficient robot navigation in crowds requires anticipating pedestrian motion despite uncertain and potentially shifting prediction errors. Existing reactive methods can produce oscillatory behavior, while predictive planners often treat forecasts as exact or rely on restrictive error models. Incorporating conservative uncertainty sets as hard constraints can also render model predictive control (MPC) infeasible. We propose \textit{CoCoNav}, a crowd-navigation framework that combines online conformal calibration with runtime-certified planning. A horizon-specific conformal proportional--integral controller adapts trajectory-error bounds to regulate long-run empirical coverage, enabling the framework to respond to changing prediction errors. A \textit{relax-then-verify} planner preserves solver feasibility by generating nominal trajectories with soft-constrained MPC and separately certifying them, together with contingency maneuvers, against the calibrated bounds before execution. Simulations and quadruped experiments show that CoCoNav achieves a favorable balance among collision avoidance, task success, and navigation efficiency relative to the evaluated baselines.

</details>


### [118] [Drone-Assisted UAV-UGV Collaboration for Autonomous Navigation in Snow-Covered Terrain](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07797)
*Shreyam Gupta,P. Agrawal,Priyam Gupta,R. Gautam*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种协作式UAV-UGV导航框架，用于高海拔积雪地形。采用自定义Efficient U-Net实现实时道路分割（96.5%准确率），结合EKF融合GPS/IMU实现UAV定位（误差±0.5米），通过YOLOv5+深度数据实现UGV视觉跟踪，最终通过动态路径规划在积雪环境中实现成功导航。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高海拔积雪地形中能见度低、地面不稳定，传统导航方法失效，亟需一种鲁棒且计算高效的协作式空地机器人导航方案。

Method: 1) 提出满足实时计算约束的高效U-Net架构，配合新型合成雪景数据增强技术实现道路分割；2) 使用EKF融合GPS与IMU数据进行UAV定位；3) 利用UAV的RGB-D相机通过YOLOv5视觉跟踪管道确定UGV位置；4) 基于分割结果的动态路径规划算法调整雪堆偏移，实现导航。

Result: 道路分割准确率达到96.5%；UAV最大定位误差为±0.5米；在能见度受限的测试环境中成功导航且路径偏差最小化。

Conclusion: 该协作导航框架在恶劣的高海拔积雪环境中表现出高效性和鲁棒性，验证了基于合成数据增强的U-Net分割与多传感器融合定位在实际场景中的有效性。

Abstract: This paper presents a collaborative UAV-UGV navigation framework for high-altitude, snow-covered terrain, where reduced visibility and unstable ground render conventional methods ineffective. We introduce a custom efficient U-Net architecture that falls under the computational constraints for real-time road segmentation, utilizing a novel synthetic snow data augmentation technique to achieve 96.5% segmentation accuracy. For UAV localization, we implement an Extended Kalman Filter (EKF) fusing onboard GPS and IMU data, achieving a maximum observed positional error of +-0.5 meters. The UGV position is determined via a visual tracking pipeline using YOLOv5 and depth data from the UAV's RGB-D camera. A dynamic path planning algorithm utilizes this segmentation to adjust for snow drifts, enabling successful navigation in obscured test environment with minimal deviation.

</details>


### [119] [A Mixed-Stiffness Anthropomimetic Fingertip Broadens the Operating Range for Coin Grasping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07887)
*Kaigen Go,Yinlai Jiang,Hiroshi Yokoi,Shunta Togo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文研究机器人在硬表面上抓取薄扁物体（如硬币）的方法，提出通过在软指垫内通过材料选择而非解剖结构构建额外约束边界来扩展抓取工作范围。实验制造了中心为Shore E10硅胶、两侧为Shore A60硅胶的拟人指尖，与均匀软E10指尖对比，在斜面旋转尖端捏取（oblique rotational tip pinch）任务中，混合刚度指尖在更多测试条件下保持高成功率，但其性能优势因条件而异（如硬币沿手指轴向位置变化时均匀指尖更优）。两种指尖在调整尺寸后均能抓取日本所有六种硬币面额。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人对硬表面上薄扁物体（如硬币）的抓取仍然困难，因为传统方法需要重新定向物体、接触其底面或添加专用指甲机构。此前研究表明刚性指甲能阻止软垫变形从而形成几何约束，提高精密抓取。本文想探索能否通过在指垫内部通过材料选择（而非解剖构造）创建额外约束边界，来扩展该约束成立的抓取条件范围。

Method: 制造两种拟人指尖：(1)混合刚度指尖——中心使用Shore E10硅胶（柔软），两侧使用Shore A60硅胶（较硬）；(2)均匀E10软硅胶指尖。使用自动化装置执行斜向旋转尖端捏取动作，使指尖垫接触硬币侧表面并将其从与表面平齐、下方无缝隙的状态下提起。系统性地变化水平接近距离、垂直手指位移和食指旋转角度等参数，对比两种指尖在不同条件下的成功率。

Result: 混合刚度指尖在几何约束形成和向稳定抓取过渡阶段均比均匀指尖在更多测试设置下保持高成功率。无指甲指尖对在所有36个实验1-1条件下均失败。然而，当硬币沿手指轴向位置变化时，均匀指尖表现更好，表明这是条件依赖性权衡。经过针对硬币尺寸的调整后，两种指尖都能抓取日本全部六种硬币面额。

Conclusion: 薄物体抓取的操作范围不仅取决于指垫软度，还取决于刚度在带指甲支撑指垫内的放置位置。边界放置位置是一个候选的指尖设计变量，可作为模块化设计参数用于扩展机器人精密抓取薄扁物体的能力。

Abstract: Robotic grasping of thin, flat objects such as coins on hard surfaces remains challenging because conventional methods require reorienting the object, accessing its underside, or adding a dedicated nail mechanism. We previously showed that a rigid nail arrests soft-pad deformation and thereby forms a geometric constraint that improves precision grasping. Here we asked whether an additional constraint-forming boundary, created within the pad by material choice rather than by anatomy, could extend the conditions under which that constraint holds. We fabricated anthropomimetic fingertips with Shore E10 silicone at the center and Shore A60 at the sides, and compared them with uniformly soft E10 fingertips. An automated apparatus performed an oblique rotational tip pinch in which the pad engaged the coin's lateral surface, lifting it from flush contact with no gap beneath it. Over variations in horizontal approach distances, vertical finger displacements, and index-finger rotation, the mixed-stiffness pair maintained high success rates across more tested settings than the uniform pair during both geometric-constraint formation and the transition to a stable grasp. The nail-free pair failed in all 36 conditions of Experiment 1-1. However, the uniform pair performed better when coin position along the finger axis was varied, a condition-dependent trade-off. After tuning for coin size, both fingertip types grasped all six Japanese denominations. These results suggest that the operating range for thin-object grasping depends not only on pad softness but also on where stiffness is placed within a nail-supported pad, making boundary placement a candidate fingertip design variable.

</details>


### [120] [Auditing Instruction-Trajectory Mismatches in Multimodal Robot Demonstrations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07895)
*Simon Holk,Ryosuke Takanami,Tatsuya Matsushima,Yusuke Iwasawa,Yutaka Matsuo,Yueh-Hua Wu,Kei Ota*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出MMPF（多模态概率融合）方法，用于检测和修正视觉-语言-动作策略训练数据中的指令-轨迹不匹配（ITM）问题。该方法无需训练，将每个模态视为专家，结合局部邻域一致性和全局原型相似性估计任务标签分布，并通过预测熵加权进行专家乘积融合。在LIBERO基准和真实机器人数据上，MMPF在ITM检测和标签修正方面表现最佳，并显著提升了下游策略学习性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人演示数据集中存在一种隐蔽且有害的失败模式：轨迹行为正确但与语言指令不匹配（ITM）。由于这类数据的轨迹看起来合理，容易被忽视，但会破坏策略学到的语言-行为映射关系。现有的审计方法无法有效处理这类问题，因此需要一种能够检测并修正ITM的方法。

Method: 提出了MMPF（Multimodal Probabilistic Fusion）审计框架，核心创新包括：(1) 将每个模态视为独立专家；(2) 通过局部邻域一致性和全局原型相似性估计任务标签分布；(3) 使用预测熵加权在专家乘积（product of experts）框架下融合各模态信息。整个方法无需额外训练，可直接应用于现有数据集。

Result: 在LIBERO基准测试（注入指令不匹配）和含噪声的真实机器人数据上，MMPF在ITM检测和标签修正准确性方面取得了最佳综合性能。审计后的数据在多数依赖语言消歧任务的下游策略学习中显著提升了性能。真实机器人实验验证了该方法能改善策略表现，并揭示了过滤演示与重新标注两种处理策略之间的权衡。

Conclusion: ITM是机器人演示数据中不可忽视的隐蔽数据质量问题，MMPF作为一种训练无关的审计框架能够有效检测和修正这类问题。方法在基准和真实场景中均展现优势，且在实际使用中需要根据具体需求权衡过滤与重标注两种数据修复策略。

Abstract: Robot demonstration datasets used to train vision-language-action policies can contain a subtle but harmful failure mode: trajectories that are behaviorally correct but paired with the wrong language instruction. We study post-hoc auditing of these Instruction-Trajectory Mismatches (ITMs). Unlike failed rollouts, ITMs often look plausible, and can corrupt the language-behavior mapping learned by the policy. We propose Multimodal Probabilistic Fusion (MMPF), a training-free auditing framework that treats each modality as an expert, estimates a task-label distribution from local neighborhood agreement and global prototype similarity, and then fuses modalities with predictive-entropy weighting in a product of experts. Across LIBERO benchmarks with injected instruction mismatches and noisy real-robot data, MMPF achieves the strongest overall ITM detection and label correction accuracy. We also show that auditing improves most downstream policy learning in settings where language is needed to disambiguate the task. We demonstrate in real robot experiments that our method can achieve improved policy performance and show the trade-off of filtering demonstrations compared to relabeling.

</details>


### [121] [4D-WAM: Infusing Spatiotemporal Awareness into World Action Models through Trajectory Fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08023)
*Lishan Yang,Wenxuan Song,Xi Wang,Pingyue Sheng,Zheng Fang,Ziyang Zhou,Junjie He,Haodong Yan,Jiayi Chen,Nan Sun,Qiao Sun,Lingqiao Liu,Yan Wang,Yuxiang Gao,Feras Dayoub,Haoang Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出4D-WAM，一种模型无关的训练策略，通过表示对齐将3D轨迹场的时空知识注入世界动作模型（WAMs），使模型从2D像素空间扩展到4D时空感知，从而提升动作生成和视频预测能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有世界动作模型（WAMs）通常在2D像素空间中表示视频，与机器人动作执行的3D空间存在表示差距。虽然近期的3D方法引入了3D信息，但未能充分利用3D结构的动力学特性。因此需要一种方法弥补2D表示与3D执行之间的鸿沟，同时充分利用3D结构的动态信息。

Method: 提出4D-WAM，一种模型无关的训练策略，通过将3D轨迹场的时空知识通过表示对齐注入WAMs。包含两个互补目标：1) 运动对齐（motion alignment），对齐相邻帧间的时序特征变化，鼓励模型在训练期间建立局部4D感知；2) 目的地对齐（destination alignment），通过最小化源帧与其注意力相似度分布之间的差距，引导模型从源帧推断最终目的地。这两个目标共同提供局部运动监督和长程目标指导。

Result: 在多种基础模型上的分布内和分布外实验中，该方法在空间理解、执行精度、鲁棒性、泛化能力和多样性方面均表现出改进。

Conclusion: 4D-WAM通过局部运动监督与长程目标引导相结合，使WAMs能够学习轨迹级别的时空表示，有效弥合了2D像素表示与3D动作执行之间的鸿沟。

Abstract: Building on recent advances in world models, World Action Models (WAMs) jointly model video prediction and action generation. However, they typically represent videos in 2D pixel space, creating a representation gap with 3D space in which robotic actions are executed. Recent 3D approaches introduce 3D information, but fail to fully exploit the dynamics of 3D structures. In this work, we propose 4D-WAM, a model-agnostic training strategy that injects spatiotemporal knowledge from 3D trajectory fields into WAMs through representation alignment. To this end, we introduce two complementary objectives: 1) motion alignment, which aligns temporal feature variations across adjacent frames and encourages the model to build local 4D awareness during training, and 2) destination alignment, which guides the model to infer the final destination from the source frame by minimizing the gap between their attention-like similarity distributions. Together, these objectives provide both local motion supervision and long-horizon goal guidance, enabling WAMs to learn trajectory-level spatiotemporal representations. Extensive in-distribution and out-of-distribution experiments across different base models demonstrate the model's improvements in spatial understanding, execution precision, robustness, generalization, and versatility.

</details>


### [122] [DA-NBV: A Direction-Aware Next-Best-View Planner for Efficient 3D Reconstruction of Ships at Sea](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08025)
*Jiaming Chen,Juntao Yang,Zhentao Zou,Qi Ming,Yi Yu,Zhihang Zhong,Xue Yang,Xue Jiang,Yue Zhou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出DA-NBV，一种方向感知的下一最佳视角（NBV）规划策略，通过将方向观测统计信息引入传统占用状态中，并结合可学习的位置优势场（PAF），解决海上船舶因结构复杂、自遮挡严重和波浪扰动导致的三维重建不完整问题，在重建完整性和精度上均优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 海上船舶的精确三维重建对海事监管、损伤评估和自主航行至关重要。然而，高质量数据采集依赖人工设计的轨迹或操作员，成本高且难以扩展。现有NBV策略主要建模空间占用，忽略了方向性观测历史，导致面对船舶复杂的上层建筑和严重自遮挡时重建不完整。此外，海上波浪引起的升沉、横摇和纵摇持续改变船舶姿态和表面可见性，加上风力扰动和有限的机载功率，对扫描效率提出了更高要求。

Method: DA-NBV是一种方向感知的NBV策略，其核心创新包括：(1) 在传统占用状态基础上增强方向观测统计信息；(2) 提出可学习的Position Advantage Field（PAF），利用方向信息引导视点选择；(3) 采用局部约束的动作空间和非线性覆盖塑形奖励以提升扫描效率；(4) 开发船舶专用的SeaShip-3D数据集和可配置的海况仿真环境。

Result: 在多种升沉、横摇和纵摇条件下的实验中，DA-NBV将重建完整性提升约3个百分点，Chamfer距离降低43%，同时实现了更高的路径效率。

Conclusion: DA-NBV通过引入方向感知机制有效解决了海上船舶重建中因自遮挡和姿态变化导致的视点覆盖不足问题，显著提升了重建质量与扫描效率，为海上自主三维采集提供了可行的自动化方案。

Abstract: Accurate 3D reconstruction of ships at sea is important for maritime supervision, damage assessment, and autonomous maritime operations. Although 3D reconstruction has advanced considerably, high-quality data acquisition still largely relies on manually designed trajectories or skilled operators, resulting in high costs and limited scalability. Next-best-view (NBV) planning automates this process by selecting subsequent viewpoints based on the current state. However, existing NBV policies mainly model spatial occupancy while overlooking directional observation history. This limitation is particularly problematic for ships: their complex superstructures and severe self-occlusions require observations from multiple viewpoints, and insufficient directional coverage often yields incomplete reconstructions. These challenges are further amplified at sea, where wave-induced heave, roll, and pitch continuously alter the ship's pose and surface visibility. Meanwhile, wind disturbances and limited onboard power impose stricter requirements on scanning efficiency. To address these challenges, we propose DA-NBV, a direction-aware NBV policy that augments the conventional occupancy state with directional observation statistics. We introduce a learnable Position Advantage Field (PAF) that uses directional information to guide viewpoint selection. The policy further adopts a locally constrained action space and a nonlinear coverage-shaping reward to improve scanning efficiency. We also develop the ship-oriented SeaShip-3D dataset and a configurable sea-state simulation environment. Experiments under varying heave, roll, and pitch conditions show that DA-NBV improves reconstruction completeness by approximately 3 percentage points and reduces Chamfer distance by 43% while achieving higher path efficiency.

</details>


### [123] [Compiling and Benchmarking Task-State Horizons for Embodied Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08036)
*Meiqi Wang,Shichao Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出任务状态地平线（TSH）概念来衡量智能体需追踪的任务相关状态转换跨度，并引入RoboGraph——一个将状态转换依赖编译为可执行符号图的机器人任务编译器，构建了84个场景、588个回合的基准测试。对15个先进智能体模型的评估显示，大多数模型在长TSH场景下表现不佳，暴露出在长期任务中维护、探索和更新任务相关状态的显著短板。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器人基准主要用动作序列长度和子任务复杂性来衡量任务难度，但忽略了另一个关键挑战：智能体必须追踪由自身探索和环境动态引起的不断演变的、与任务相关的世界状态。这种状态追踪跨度（即TSH）是现有评估体系未覆盖的维度。

Method: 提出了RoboGraph，一个机器人任务编译器，将空间和时间因果依赖转换为可执行的符号图来构建任务状态地平线。RoboGraph能够捕捉包括执行过程中意外失败和干预导致的状态转换依赖。基于此构建了84个场景、588个回合、具有不同TSH的基准测试，并在语义和视觉闭环环境中评估了15种先进的智能体模型。

Result: 实验表明大多数先进模型在面对高TSH需求时表现困难，揭示了它们在长时间任务中维持、探索和更新任务相关状态方面的显著差距。

Conclusion: 任务状态地平线是一个有别于传统难度度量的关键维度，现有智能体模型在此维度上存在系统性不足，需要未来研究更关注状态追踪能力而非仅关注动作序列长度和子任务复杂度。

Abstract: Frontier agentic models are increasingly deployed as high-level planners for long-horizon embodied tasks. Existing robotic benchmarks have advanced long-horizon evaluation, but primarily characterize difficulty through action-sequence length and subtask complexity, overlooking a distinct challenge: agents must track evolving task-relevant world states induced by both their exploration and environmental dynamics. We define the span of task-relevant state transitions that an agent must track as task-state horizon (TSH). To evaluate how agent performance varies with TSH, we introduce RoboGraph, a robotic task compiler that translates state-transition dependencies into executable symbolic graphs. Specifically, RoboGraph constructs task-state horizons from spatial and temporal causal dependencies, including those induced by unexpected failures and interventions during task execution. Building on RoboGraph, we release a benchmark comprising 588 episodes across 84 scenes with varying TSHs. Experiments evaluating 15 advanced agentic models in both semantic and visual closed-loop environments show that most models struggle with demanding TSHs, revealing substantial gaps in maintaining, exploring, and updating task-relevant state over long horizon.

</details>
